Motivation: 现有价值观数据集大多忽略行为者角色、缺乏事件结构、依赖孤立语句或合成场景，难以支持在真实新闻语境下的价值观理解。因此需要构建一个更贴近现实、结构化且具备价值方向标注的基准数据集。

Method: 提出NEVU基准，基于真实新闻文章，定义四个语义层级（子事件、行为复合事件、故事复合事件、全文），对（语义单元, 行为者）对进行标注，标注内容包括价值观线索、归属行为者及价值方向。采用LLM辅助标注结合分阶段验证与人工审核的方式构建数据集。使用包含54个细粒度值和20个粗粒度类别的层次化价值体系。

Result: 构建了包含45,793个（单元, 行为者）对和168,061个有向价值实例的数据集。提供了专有和开源大模型的统一基线结果，发现轻量级适配方法（如LoRA）可显著提升开源模型表现。

Conclusion: NEVU为新闻文本中的价值观理解提供了一个更真实、结构化和精细化的评估基准，不仅能用于模型评测，还能支持监督学习下的模型适配，推动价值观对齐研究向更复杂、现实的场景发展。

Abstract: Existing human value datasets do not directly support value understanding in factual news: many are actor-agnostic, rely on isolated utterances or synthetic scenarios, and lack explicit event structure or value direction. We present \textbf{NEVU} (\textbf{N}ews \textbf{E}vent-centric \textbf{V}alue \textbf{U}nderstanding), a benchmark for \emph{actor-conditioned}, \emph{event-centric}, and \emph{direction-aware} human value recognition in factual news. NEVU evaluates whether models can identify value cues, attribute them to the correct actor, and determine value direction from grounded evidence. Built from 2{,}865 English news articles, NEVU organizes annotations at four semantic unit levels (\textbf{Subevent}, \textbf{behavior-based composite event}, \textbf{story-based composite event}, and \textbf{Article}) and labels \mbox{(unit, actor)} pairs for fine-grained evaluation across local and composite contexts. The annotations are produced through an LLM-assisted pipeline with staged verification and targeted human auditing. Using a hierarchical value space with \textbf{54} fine-grained values and \textbf{20} coarse-grained categories, NEVU covers 45{,}793 unit--actor pairs and 168{,}061 directed value instances. We provide unified baselines for proprietary and open-source LLMs, and find that lightweight adaptation (LoRA) consistently improves open-source models, showing that although NEVU is designed primarily as a benchmark, it also supports supervised adaptation beyond prompting-only evaluation. Data availability is described in Appendix~\ref{app:data_code_availability}.

</details>


### [154] [How do LLMs Compute Verbal Confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17839)
*Dharshan Kumaran,Arthur Conmy,Federico Barbero,Simon Osindero,Viorica Patraucean,Petar Velickovic*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明，大语言模型中的言语置信度反映的是自动且复杂的自我评估过程，而非事后重建，其置信度表征在回答生成时即被缓存，并包含超出词元对数概率的丰富信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型如何生成言语置信度，以及这种置信度是基于简单的词元概率还是更丰富的答案质量评估。

Method: 通过激活引导、补丁、噪声注入、交换实验和注意力阻断等方法，分析Gemma 3 27B和Qwen 2.5 7B模型中置信度的生成时机与信息流，并使用线性探测和方差分解评估置信度表征的内容。

Result: 发现置信度表征在回答临近位置提前出现并被缓存，信息从答案词元流向首个回答后位置并被检索输出；这些缓存表征能显著解释言语置信度的方差，超越词元对数概率的预测能力。

Conclusion: 言语置信度体现的是模型在生成答案时自动进行的复杂自我评估，具有内在一致性和认知深度，为理解大模型的元认知机制和提升校准性提供了依据。

Abstract: Verbal confidence -- prompting LLMs to state their confidence as a number or category -- is widely used to extract uncertainty estimates from black-box models. However, how LLMs internally generate such scores remains unknown. We address two questions: first, when confidence is computed - just-in-time when requested, or automatically during answer generation and cached for later retrieval; and second, what verbal confidence represents - token log-probabilities, or a richer evaluation of answer quality? Focusing on Gemma 3 27B and Qwen 2.5 7B, we provide convergent evidence for cached retrieval. Activation steering, patching, noising, and swap experiments reveal that confidence representations emerge at answer-adjacent positions before appearing at the verbalization site. Attention blocking pinpoints the information flow: confidence is gathered from answer tokens, cached at the first post-answer position, then retrieved for output. Critically, linear probing and variance partitioning reveal that these cached representations explain substantial variance in verbal confidence beyond token log-probabilities, suggesting a richer answer-quality evaluation rather than a simple fluency readout. These findings demonstrate that verbal confidence reflects automatic, sophisticated self-evaluation -- not post-hoc reconstruction -- with implications for understanding metacognition in LLMs and improving calibration.

</details>


### [155] [Mitigating LLM Hallucinations through Domain-Grounded Tiered Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17872)
*Md. Asraful Haque,Aasar Mehdi,Maaz Mahboob,Tamkeen Fatima*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于领域知识的分层检索与验证架构，通过四阶段流程提升大语言模型的事实准确性，实验证明其在多个基准上显著优于零样本基线，但在“错误前提过度断言”方面仍存在挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽然流畅，但容易产生事实性错误（幻觉），在高风险领域中可靠性不足，因此需要一种系统性机制来检测和纠正这些错误。

Method: 构建一个四阶段、自调节的流水线：1）内在验证与早退机制优化计算；2）基于领域检测器的自适应搜索路由；3）纠正性文档评分（CRAG）过滤无关上下文；4）外在再生与原子级声明验证，使用LangGraph实现。

Result: 在五个基准的650个查询中测试，该系统在所有环境中均优于零样本基线，TimeQA v2和MMLU Global Facts上的胜率达83.7%和78.0%，事实回答的 groundedness 得分稳定在78.8%至86.4%之间，但发现‘错误前提过度断言’的持续失效模式。

Conclusion: 该架构能有效防止错误信息传播，为提升大语言模型可靠性提供了可行路径，未来工作应优先引入预检索的‘可回答性’判断节点以进一步缩小可靠性差距。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved unprecedented fluency but remain susceptible to "hallucinations" - the generation of factually incorrect or ungrounded content. This limitation is particularly critical in high-stakes domains where reliability is paramount. We propose a domain-grounded tiered retrieval and verification architecture designed to systematically intercept factual inaccuracies by shifting LLMs from stochastic pattern-matchers to verified truth-seekers. The proposed framework utilizes a four-phase, self-regulating pipeline implemented via LangGraph: (I) Intrinsic Verification with Early-Exit logic to optimize compute, (II) Adaptive Search Routing utilizing a Domain Detector to target subject-specific archives, (III) Corrective Document Grading (CRAG) to filter irrelevant context, and (IV) Extrinsic Regeneration followed by atomic claim-level verification. The system was evaluated across 650 queries from five diverse benchmarks: TimeQA v2, FreshQA v2, HaluEval General, MMLU Global Facts, and TruthfulQA. Empirical results demonstrate that the pipeline consistently outperforms zero-shot baselines across all environments. Win rates peaked at 83.7% in TimeQA v2 and 78.0% in MMLU Global Facts, confirming high efficacy in domains requiring granular temporal and numerical precision. Groundedness scores remained robustly stable between 78.8% and 86.4% across factual-answer rows. While the architecture provides a robust fail-safe for misinformation, a persistent failure mode of "False-Premise Overclaiming" was identified. These findings provide a detailed empirical characterization of multi-stage RAG behavior and suggest that future work should prioritize pre-retrieval "answerability" nodes to further bridge the reliability gap in conversational AI.

</details>


### [156] [DebugLM: Learning Traceable Training Data Provenance for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17884)
*Wenjie Jacky Mo,Qin Liu,Xiaofei Wen,Wenxuan Zhou,Zhe Zhao,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DebugLM是一个为大语言模型提供内置数据溯源的框架，能够将模型行为精确追溯到特定训练数据源，并支持无需重训练的目标化测试时修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏对大型语言模型行为来源的可观测性，导致调试困难且错误在分布变化或模型更新后容易重现。

Method: 通过在多阶段训练管道中引入唯一的数据溯源标签，使模型学会将其响应与负责的数据集关联，从而实现行为追踪和测试时针对性修正。

Result: 实验表明DebugLM能准确追踪多阶段训练中的行为来源，有效实现测试时修复，同时保持模型的整体性能。

Conclusion: DebugLM提升了大型语言模型的可解释性和可控性，为开发者提供了高效调试和管理模型行为的新工具。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are trained through multi-stage pipelines over heterogeneous data sources, yet developers lack a principled way to pinpoint the specific data responsible for an observed behavior. This lack of observability reduces debugging to reactive patching and makes failures prone to recur under distribution shift or subsequent model updates. To address this limitation, we propose DebugLM, a framework that equips LLMs with built-in data provenance, enabling them to explicitly trace the origins of their behaviors to specific training data sources. Specifically, the model learns to associate its responses with unique provenance tags that indicate the responsible dataset, empowering developers to precisely identify where undesirable behaviors are learned. Building on this capability, DebugLM further supports targeted test-time remediation, enabling developers to selectively trigger targeted refusal for specified data sources without retraining or modifying model parameters. Experiments demonstrate that DebugLM provides accurate behavior tracing in multi-stage training pipelines and effective test-time remediation while preserving the general utility of the model.

</details>


### [157] [IndicSafe: A Benchmark for Evaluating Multilingual LLM Safety in South Asia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17915)
*Priyaranjan Pattnayak,Sanchari Chowdhuri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究首次系统评估了10种主流大语言模型在12种印度语言中的安全性表现，发现跨语言安全行为存在显著差异，安全对齐效果在不同语言间未能均匀迁移。为此，作者发布了首个面向印度语言的 culturally grounded 安全评测基准 IndicSafe，并呼吁采用基于区域风险的语言适配对齐策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在多语言环境中的部署，其在文化多样且资源较少的语言中的安全性行为尚不明确，尤其是在代表超过12亿人口但训练数据中代表性不足的印度语言中，亟需系统性评估其安全对齐的泛化能力。

Method: 研究构建了一个包含6000个涵盖种姓、宗教、性别、健康和政治等领域的文化相关提示的数据集，对10种主流大语言模型在12种印度语言的翻译版本上进行安全性评估，并使用提示级熵、类别偏差分数和多语言一致性指数量化模型表现。

Result: 跨语言一致性仅为12.8%，安全响应率的方差超过17%；部分模型在低资源语种中过度拒绝良性提示或过度标记敏感话题，而另一些则未能识别不安全输出，暴露出严重的安全泛化缺陷。

Conclusion: 大语言模型的安全对齐不能直接跨语言迁移，尤其在文化复杂且资源有限的语言中存在显著漏洞，必须发展考虑区域文化背景和本地化风险的语言适配安全策略。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are deployed in multilingual settings, their safety behavior in culturally diverse, low-resource languages remains poorly understood. We present the first systematic evaluation of LLM safety across 12 Indic languages, spoken by over 1.2 billion people but underrepresented in LLM training data. Using a dataset of 6,000 culturally grounded prompts spanning caste, religion, gender, health, and politics, we assess 10 leading LLMs on translated variants of the prompt.
  Our analysis reveals significant safety drift: cross-language agreement is just 12.8\%, and \texttt{SAFE} rate variance exceeds 17\% across languages. Some models over-refuse benign prompts in low-resource scripts, overflag politically sensitive topics, while others fail to flag unsafe generations. We quantify these failures using prompt-level entropy, category bias scores, and multilingual consistency indices.
  Our findings highlight critical safety generalization gaps in multilingual LLMs and show that safety alignment does not transfer evenly across languages. We release \textsc{IndicSafe}, the first benchmark to enable culturally informed safety evaluation for Indic deployments, and advocate for language-aware alignment strategies grounded in regional harms.

</details>


### [158] [Efficient Training-Free Multi-Token Prediction via Embedding-Space Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17942)
*Raghavv Goel,Mukul Gagrani,Mingu Lee,Chris Lott*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的多令牌预测方法，通过在LLM中动态插入掩码令牌实现未来令牌的并行预测，提升生成吞吐量而不损失精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型仅被训练为逐个生成下一个令牌，但其具备潜在的多令牌预测能力，如何在不修改模型权重或引入辅助模型的情况下挖掘这一能力是本文的研究动机。

Method: 通过从嵌入空间动态采样掩码令牌来探测LLM，构建基于top-K候选的推测令牌树，并采用轻量级剪枝策略保留高概率延续路径，最后并行验证候选以实现解码加速。

Result: 在LLaMA3和Qwen3等模型上，相比现有无需训练的方法，接受长度分别提升约12%和8-12%，吞吐量提高达15-19%，且保持无损生成。

Conclusion: 解码器层能自然对齐掩码令牌表示与下一令牌状态，使得无需重训练或辅助模型即可实现准确的多步预测，验证了掩码探测作为有效MTP手段的可行性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit latent multi-token prediction (MTP) capabilities despite being trained solely for next-token generation. We propose a simple, training-free MTP approach that probes an LLM using on-the-fly mask tokens drawn from its embedding space, enabling parallel prediction of future tokens without modifying model weights or relying on auxiliary draft models. Our method constructs a speculative token tree by sampling top-K candidates from mask-token logits and applies a lightweight pruning strategy to retain high-probability continuations. During decoding, candidate predictions are verified in parallel, resulting in lossless generation while substantially reducing the number of model calls and improving token throughput. Across benchmarks, our probing-based MTP consistently outperforms existing training-free baselines, increasing acceptance length by approximately 12\% on LLaMA3 and 8--12\% on Qwen3, and achieving throughput gains of up to 15--19\%. Finally, we provide theoretical insights and empirical evidence showing that decoder layers naturally align mask-token representations with next-token states, enabling accurate multi-step prediction without retraining or auxiliary models.

</details>


### [159] [ShapleyLaw: A Game-Theoretic Approach to Multilingual Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17945)
*Xuyang Cao,Qianying Liu,Chuan Xiao,Yusuke Oda,Pontus Stenetorp,Daisuke Kawahara,Makoto Onizuka,Sadao Kurohashi,Shuyuan Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于合作博弈论的多语言缩放定律ShapleyLaw，通过量化每种语言在跨语言迁移中的贡献来优化多语言预训练中的语言混合比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多语言缩放定律未考虑跨语言迁移效应，导致语言混合比例次优，因此需要一种能准确衡量语言间相互影响的方法。

Method: 将多语言预训练建模为合作博弈，每种语言作为一个参与者，利用Shapley值量化各语言对测试损失减少的贡献，从而提出ShapleyLaw模型。

Result: 实验表明，ShapleyLaw在模型性能预测和语言混合比例优化方面优于基线方法。

Conclusion: ShapleyLaw能够更准确地预测多语言模型表现，并有效优化预训练数据中的语言配比，提升了多语言模型的效率与性能。

Abstract: In multilingual pretraining, the test loss of a pretrained model is heavily influenced by the proportion of each language in the pretraining data, namely the \textit{language mixture ratios}. Multilingual scaling laws can predict the test loss under different language mixture ratios and can therefore be used to estimate the optimal ratios. However, the current approaches to multilingual scaling laws do not measure the \textit{cross-lingual transfer} effect, resulting in suboptimal mixture ratios. In this paper, we consider multilingual pretraining as a cooperative game in which each language acts as a player that jointly contributes to pretraining, gaining the resulting reduction in test loss as the payoff. Consequently, from the perspective of cooperative game theory, we quantify the cross-lingual transfer from each language by its contribution in the game, and propose a game-theoretic multilingual scaling law called \textit{ShapleyLaw}. Our experiments show that ShapleyLaw outperforms baseline methods in model performance prediction and language mixture optimization.

</details>


### [160] [Gender Disambiguation in Machine Translation: Diagnostic Evaluation in Decoder-Only Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17952)
*Chiara Manna,Hosein Mohebbi,Afra Alishahi,Frédéric Blain,Eva Vanmassenhove*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的度量方法“先验偏差”，用于评估解码器-only机器翻译模型中的性别偏见，并发现后训练能减少男性先验偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于语言中性别标记的系统性差异，机器翻译常需将隐含的源信号明确化为性别标记形式，而标准基准测试无法全面反映现代机器翻译中的性别偏见复杂性。

Method: 引入“先验偏差”这一新度量标准，并将其应用于解码器-only的机器翻译模型中，扩展了现有的偏见评估框架。

Result: 研究表明，尽管规模庞大且处于技术前沿，解码器-only模型在性别特定指标上并未普遍优于编码器-解码器架构；但后训练（如指令调优）不仅能提高上下文感知能力，还能减少男性先验偏差。

Conclusion: 后训练策略有助于缓解机器翻译模型中的性别偏见，特别是在降低默认男性假设方面表现显著。

Abstract: While Large Language Models achieve state-of-the-art results across a wide range of NLP tasks, they remain prone to systematic biases. Among these, gender bias is particularly salient in MT, due to systematic differences across languages in whether and how gender is marked. As a result, translation often requires disambiguating implicit source signals into explicit gender-marked forms. In this context, standard benchmarks may capture broad disparities but fail to reflect the full complexity of gender bias in modern MT. In this paper, we extend recent frameworks on bias evaluation by: (i) introducing a novel measure coined "Prior Bias", capturing a model's default gender assumptions, and (ii) applying the framework to decoder-only MT models. Our results show that, despite their scale and state-of-the-art status, decoder-only models do not generally outperform encoder-decoder architectures on gender-specific metrics; however, post-training (e.g., instruction tuning) not only improves contextual awareness but also reduces the masculine Prior Bias.

</details>


### [161] [ConGA: Guidelines for Contextual Gender Annotation. A Framework for Annotating Gender in Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17962)
*Argentina Anna Rescigno,Eva Vanmassenhove,Johanna Monti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为ConGA的上下文性别标注框架，用于解决从无性别语言（如英语）到有形态性别语言（如意大利语）翻译中的性别偏见问题，并通过标注gENder-IT数据集揭示了当前机器翻译系统中男性形式过度使用的系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于英语等语言缺乏语法性别标记，而意大利语等语言要求语法上的性别一致，导致机器翻译系统在翻译时倾向于默认使用阳性形式，从而引发性别偏见。本文旨在解决这一跨语言性别表达的不对称问题。

Method: 提出了一套基于语言学的词级别性别标注框架ConGA，区分英语中的语义性别（阳性M、阴性F、模糊A）和意大利语中的语法性别实现（M/F），并引入实体级标识符以实现跨句追踪。该框架应用于gENder-IT数据集，构建了评估翻译中性别偏见的黄金标准资源。

Result: 应用ConGA后发现现有机器翻译系统存在系统性的阳性偏好和不一致的阴性形式生成问题，表明当前MT系统在处理性别表达方面仍有显著缺陷。

Conclusion: ConGA提供了一个方法论和基准，有助于推动更具备性别意识和多语言能力的自然语言处理系统的发展。

Abstract: Handling gender across languages remains a persistent challenge for Machine Translation (MT) and Large Language Models (LLMs), especially when translating from gender-neutral languages into morphologically gendered ones, such as English to Italian. English largely omits grammatical gender, while Italian requires explicit agreement across multiple grammatical categories. This asymmetry often leads MT systems to default to masculine forms, reinforcing bias and reducing translation accuracy. To address this issue, we present the Contextual Gender Annotation (ConGA) framework, a linguistically grounded set of guidelines for word-level gender annotation. The scheme distinguishes between semantic gender in English through three tags, Masculine (M), Feminine (F), and Ambiguous (A), and grammatical gender realisation in Italian (Masculine (M), Feminine (F)), combined with entity-level identifiers for cross-sentence tracking. We apply ConGA to the gENder-IT dataset, creating a gold-standard resource for evaluating gender bias in translation. Our results reveal systematic masculine overuse and inconsistent feminine realisation, highlighting persistent limitations of current MT systems. By combining fine-grained linguistic annotation with quantitative evaluation, this work offers both a methodology and a benchmark for building more gender-aware and multilingual NLP systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [Generative AI-assisted Participatory Modeling in Socio-Environmental Planning under Deep Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17021)
*Zhihao Pei,Nir Lipovetzky,Angela M. Rojas-Arevalo,Fjalar J. de Haan,Enayat A. Moallemi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型的模板化工作流程，用于支持在深度不确定性下的社会环境规划中的问题概念化过程，通过参与式建模将利益相关者自然语言描述转化为定量模型，并在湖泊问题和电力市场问题中验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在社会环境规划中，问题概念化通常依赖于耗时且复杂的参与式建模过程，需要将利益相关者的自然语言描述转化为定量模型，而这一过程亟需高效工具支持。

Method: 提出一种基于大语言模型（如ChatGPT 5.2 Instant）的模板化工作流，自动识别利益相关者描述中的关键模型组件，整合多元视角，构建统一模型，并通过人机迭代优化实现Python建模。

Result: 在湖泊问题和电力市场问题的应用中，经过少数几次迭代并结合人工验证，均获得了可接受的模型输出结果。

Conclusion: 大语言模型可作为提升社会环境规划中问题概念化效率的有效工具，有助于加速后续的政策探索与规划部署。

Abstract: Socio-environmental planning under deep uncertainty requires researchers to identify and conceptualize problems before exploring policies and deploying plans. In practice and model-based planning approaches, this problem conceptualization process often relies on participatory modeling to translate stakeholders' natural-language descriptions into a quantitative model, making this process complex and time-consuming. To facilitate this process, we propose a templated workflow that uses large language models for an initial conceptualization process. During the workflow, researchers can use large language models to identify the essential model components from stakeholders' intuitive problem descriptions, explore their diverse perspectives approaching the problem, assemble these components into a unified model, and eventually implement the model in Python through iterative communication. These results will facilitate the subsequent socio-environmental planning under deep uncertainty steps. Using ChatGPT 5.2 Instant, we demonstrated this workflow on the lake problem and an electricity market problem, both of which demonstrate socio-environmental planning problems. In both cases, acceptable outputs were obtained after a few iterations with human verification and refinement. These experiments indicated that large language models can serve as an effective tool for facilitating participatory modeling in the problem conceptualization process in socio-environmental planning.

</details>


### [163] [Cascade-Aware Multi-Agent Routing: Spatio-Temporal Sidecars and Geometry-Switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17112)
*Davide Di Gioia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于动态执行图中路径风险估计的在线几何控制方法，通过引入几何感知的调度机制，显著降低了树状和循环结构中任务失败的传播风险，在Genesis 3基准上实现了从50.4%到87.2%的整体胜率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI推理系统调度器在任务委派图中忽略拓扑几何特性，导致无法有效应对不同结构（如树状与环状）中故障传播模式的差异，尤其在树状结构中易引发级联失效，本文旨在填补这一可观察性空白并量化其系统代价。

Method: 提出一种结合时空传播模型与学习型几何选择器的在线几何控制方法：包括欧氏时空传播基线、具有时间衰减和突发激励的双曲路径风险模型，以及基于MLP的几何选择器；选择器利用六项拓扑统计量和三项几何感知信号（BFS壳增长斜率、环秩范数、拟合Poincaré曲率）进行结构判断。

Result: 在Genesis 3基准测试中，自适应切换机制将最难的非树结构胜率从64-72%提升至92%，整体胜率达87.2%；相比仅依赖bandit/LinUCB信号的基线（50.4%），性能提升36.8个百分点，树状场景增益达48-68个百分点，验证了对级联敏感性的有效缓解。

Conclusion: 一个仅133参数的轻量级sidecar模块通过引入几何感知的风险建模，显著缓解了高能力执行图系统中的几何盲区故障传播问题，证明了拓扑结构意识在AI调度系统中的关键价值。

Abstract: A common architectural pattern in advanced AI reasoning systems is the symbolic graph network: specialized agents or modules connected by delegation edges, routing tasks through a dynamic execution graph. Current schedulers optimize load and fitness but are geometry-blind: they do not model how failures propagate differently in tree-like versus cyclic regimes. In tree-like delegation, a single failure can cascade exponentially; in dense cyclic graphs, failures tend to self-limit. We identify this observability gap, quantify its system-level cost, and propose a lightweight mitigation.
  We formulate online geometry control for route-risk estimation on time-indexed execution graphs with route-local failure history. Our approach combines (i) a Euclidean spatio-temporal propagation baseline, (ii) a hyperbolic route-risk model with temporal decay (and optional burst excitation), and (iii) a learned geometry selector over structural features. The selector is a compact MLP (9->12->1) using six topology statistics plus three geometry-aware signals: BFS shell-growth slope, cycle-rank norm, and fitted Poincare curvature. On the Genesis 3 benchmark distribution, adaptive switching improves win rate in the hardest non_tree regime from 64-72% (fixed hyperbolic variants) to 92%, and achieves 87.2% overall win rate.
  To measure total system value, we compare against Genesis 3 routing without any spatio-temporal sidecar, using only native bandit/LinUCB signals (team fitness and mean node load). This baseline achieves 50.4% win rate overall and 20% in tree-like regimes; the full sidecar recovers 87.2% overall (+36.8 pp), with +48 to +68 pp gains in tree-like settings, consistent with a cascade-sensitivity analysis. Overall, a 133-parameter sidecar substantially mitigates geometry-blind failure propagation in one high-capability execution-graph system.

</details>


### [164] [Draft-and-Prune: Improving the Reliability of Auto-formalization for Logical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17233)
*Zhiyu Ni,Zheng Liang,Liangcheng Song,Chenrui Cao,Xian Zhang,Alberto Sangiovanni-Vincentelli,Pierluigi Nuzzo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Draft-and-Prune（D&P）的推理时框架，通过多样性和验证机制提升自然语言到形式化程序的自动形式化（AF）性能，在多个基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动形式化（AF）流程在实践中容易出现语法错误或语义错误，尤其是语义错误难以纠正，成为主要瓶颈。

Method: D&P首先生成多个自然语言推理草稿，并基于这些草稿生成形式化程序；然后对可执行但存在矛盾或歧义的形式化结果进行剪枝，最后通过多数投票聚合剩余路径的预测结果。

Result: 在四个基准测试（AR-LSAT、ProofWriter、PrOntoQA、LogicalDeduction）上，D&P显著提升了AF性能：在AR-LSAT上使用GPT-4和GPT-4o分别达到78.43%和78.00%准确率，远超MAD-LOGIC和CLOVER等基线方法；在PrOntoQA和LogicalDeduction上接近或达到100%准确率。

Conclusion: D&P通过引入多样性生成与语义验证机制，有效缓解了自动形式化中的语义错误问题，显著提升了逻辑推理性能，且无需额外监督。

Abstract: Auto-formalization (AF) translates natural-language reasoning problems into solver-executable programs, enabling symbolic solvers to perform sound logical deduction. In practice, however, AF pipelines are currently brittle: programs may fail to execute, or execute but encode incorrect semantics. While prior work largely mitigates syntactic failures via repairs based on solver feedback, reducing semantics failures remains a major bottleneck. We propose Draft-and-Prune (D&P), an inference-time framework that improves AF-based logical reasoning via diversity and verification. D&P first drafts multiple natural-language plans and conditions program generation on them. It further prunes executable but contradictory or ambiguous formalizations, and aggregates predictions from surviving paths via majority voting. Across four representative benchmarks (AR-LSAT, ProofWriter, PrOntoQA, LogicalDeduction), D&P substantially strengthens AF-based reasoning without extra supervision. On AR-LSAT, in the AF-only setting, D&P achieves 78.43% accuracy with GPT-4 and 78.00% accuracy with GPT-4o, significantly outperforming the strongest AF baselines MAD-LOGIC and CLOVER. D&P then attains near-ceiling performance on the other benchmarks, including 100% on PrOntoQA and LogicalDeduction.

</details>


### [165] [Contrastive Reasoning Alignment: Reinforcement Learning from Hidden Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17305)
*Haozheng Luo,Yimin Wang,Jiahao Yu,Binghui Wang,Yan Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CRAFT是一种红队对齐框架，利用模型推理能力和隐藏表示来增强对越狱攻击的鲁棒性，通过在隐状态空间中显式优化目标，实现推理级别的安全对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法主要在输出层面操作，难以应对复杂的 jailbreak 攻击，缺乏对模型内部推理过程的安全对齐。

Method: 将对比表示学习与强化学习结合，在隐藏状态空间中分离安全与不安全的推理轨迹，并引入潜文本一致性优化GRPO目标。

Result: 在多个安全基准上评估表明，CRAFT显著优于IPO和SafeKey等先进防御方法，推理安全平均提升79.0%，最终响应安全提升87.7%。

Conclusion: 通过隐空间中的推理轨迹对齐可有效提升大模型的安全性，CRAFT为构建具备内在安全意识的推理模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We propose CRAFT, a red-teaming alignment framework that leverages model reasoning capabilities and hidden representations to improve robustness against jailbreak attacks. Unlike prior defenses that operate primarily at the output level, CRAFT aligns large reasoning models to generate safety-aware reasoning traces by explicitly optimizing objectives defined over the hidden state space. Methodologically, CRAFT integrates contrastive representation learning with reinforcement learning to separate safe and unsafe reasoning trajectories, yielding a latent-space geometry that supports robust, reasoning-level safety alignment. Theoretically, we show that incorporating latent-textual consistency into GRPO eliminates superficially aligned policies by ruling them out as local optima. Empirically, we evaluate CRAFT on multiple safety benchmarks using two strong reasoning models, Qwen3-4B-Thinking and R1-Distill-Llama-8B, where it consistently outperforms state-of-the-art defenses such as IPO and SafeKey. Notably, CRAFT delivers an average 79.0% improvement in reasoning safety and 87.7% improvement in final-response safety over the base models, demonstrating the effectiveness of hidden-space reasoning alignment.

</details>


### [166] [InfoDensity: Rewarding Information-Dense Traces for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17310)
*Chengwei Wei,Jung-jae Kim,Longyin Zhang,Shengkai Chen,Nancy F. Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为InfoDensity的奖励框架，通过优化推理质量与长度的权衡，提升大模型在数学推理中的准确性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅优化最终回答长度，忽视中间推理步骤质量，容易导致奖励黑客行为；作者认为冗长是推理质量差的表现，需改进中间推理过程的质量。

Method: 基于对推理步骤中答案分布条件熵的实证研究，发现高质量推理具有低不确定性收敛和单调进展特性；据此提出InfoDensity框架，结合AUC奖励、单调性奖励，并引入长度缩放项以鼓励更简洁地实现高质量推理。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的实验表明，InfoDensity在匹配或超越现有最先进方法准确性的同时，显著减少了令牌使用量。

Conclusion: InfoDensity有效提升了推理过程的信息密度，在保证推理质量的前提下实现了更高的推理效率，为训练高效大语言模型提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) with extended reasoning capabilities often generate verbose and redundant reasoning traces, incurring unnecessary computational cost. While existing reinforcement learning approaches address this by optimizing final response length, they neglect the quality of intermediate reasoning steps, leaving models vulnerable to reward hacking. We argue that verbosity is not merely a length problem, but a symptom of poor intermediate reasoning quality. To investigate this, we conduct an empirical study tracking the conditional entropy of the answer distribution across reasoning steps. We find that high-quality reasoning traces exhibit two consistent properties: low uncertainty convergence and monotonic progress. These findings suggest that high-quality reasoning traces are informationally dense, that is, each step contributes meaningful entropy reduction relative to the total reasoning length. Motivated by this, we propose InfoDensity, a reward framework for RL training that combines an AUC-based reward and a monotonicity reward as a unified measure of reasoning quality, weighted by a length scaling term that favors achieving equivalent quality more concisely. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that InfoDensity matches or surpasses state-of-the-art baselines in accuracy while significantly reducing token usage, achieving a strong accuracy-efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [167] [Physics-informed offline reinforcement learning eliminates catastrophic fuel waste in maritime routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17319)
*Aniruddha Bora,Julie Chalfant,Chryssostomos Chryssostomidis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PIER是一个基于离线强化学习的船舶路径规划框架，利用历史数据和海洋再分析产品实现节能、安全的航行路线优化，在减少CO2排放和极端燃料消耗方面显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国际航运贡献了约3%的全球温室气体排放，但现有航线规划仍依赖启发式方法，缺乏对燃料效率与安全性的系统优化，尤其在极端情况下的表现不稳定。

Method: 提出PIER框架，结合物理信息状态构建、演示增强的离线数据和解耦的安全屏蔽机制，通过历史AIS数据和海洋再分析数据训练，无需在线仿真器即可学习高效且安全的航行策略。

Result: 在2023年一年的AIS数据验证中，PIER相比大圆航线平均减少10%的CO2排放，极端燃料消耗事件从4.8%降至0.5%，单次航行燃料方差降低3.5倍（p<0.001），平均节油区间为[2.9%, 15.7%]，且实际航迹对比显示其行为一致性高、方差低23.1倍；此外，PIER不依赖预报，在预报不确定性下性能稳定。

Conclusion: PIER提供了一种无需实时模拟和天气预报的高效、鲁棒船舶路径规划方案，显著降低了碳排放与操作风险，其架构可推广至野火疏散、飞行器轨迹优化和未知地形自主导航等领域。

Abstract: International shipping produces approximately 3% of global greenhouse gas emissions, yet voyage routing remains dominated by heuristic methods. We present PIER (Physics-Informed, Energy-efficient, Risk-aware routing), an offline reinforcement learning framework that learns fuel-efficient, safety-aware routing policies from physics-calibrated environments grounded in historical vessel tracking data and ocean reanalysis products, requiring no online simulator. Validated on one full year (2023) of AIS data across seven Gulf of Mexico routes (840 episodes per method), PIER reduces mean CO2 emissions by 10% relative to great-circle routing. However, PIER's primary contribution is eliminating catastrophic fuel waste: great-circle routing incurs extreme fuel consumption (>1.5x median) in 4.8% of voyages; PIER reduces this to 0.5%, a 9-fold reduction. Per-voyage fuel variance is 3.5x lower (p<0.001), with bootstrap 95% CI for mean savings [2.9%, 15.7%]. Partial validation against observed AIS vessel behavior confirms consistency with the fastest real transits while exhibiting 23.1x lower variance. Crucially, PIER is forecast-independent: unlike A* path optimization whose wave protection degrades 4.5x under realistic forecast uncertainty, PIER maintains constant performance using only local observations. The framework combines physics-informed state construction, demonstration-augmented offline data, and a decoupled post-hoc safety shield, an architecture that transfers to wildfire evacuation, aircraft trajectory optimization, and autonomous navigation in unmapped terrain.

</details>


### [168] [ShuttleEnv: An Interactive Data-Driven RL Environment for Badminton Strategy Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17324)
*Ang Li,Xinyang Gong,Bozhou Chen,Yunlong Lu,Jiaming Ji,Yongyi Wang,Yaodong Yang,Wenxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ShuttleEnv是一个基于精英球员比赛数据的羽毛球交互式数据驱动模拟环境，支持强化学习和快速对抗性体育中的策略行为分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在缺乏物理仿真情况下实现真实且可解释的智能体对战交互，研究快速对抗性运动中的策略决策。

Method: 利用精英球员的比赛数据构建显式的概率模型来模拟回合级动态，并开发了可重复使用的交互式仿真环境ShuttleEnv。

Result: 成功训练了多个智能体并在环境中展示了实时的、逐步可视化的羽毛球回合，支持探索不同打法风格和分析决策行为。

Conclusion: ShuttleEnv为体育人工智能中的智能体研究、可视化和演示提供了一个可复用的平台。

Abstract: We present ShuttleEnv, an interactive and data-driven simulation environment for badminton, designed to support reinforcement learning and strategic behavior analysis in fast-paced adversarial sports. The environment is grounded in elite-player match data and employs explicit probabilistic models to simulate rally-level dynamics, enabling realistic and interpretable agent-opponent interactions without relying on physics-based simulation. In this demonstration, we showcase multiple trained agents within ShuttleEnv and provide live, step-by-step visualization of badminton rallies, allowing attendees to explore different play styles, observe emergent strategies, and interactively analyze decision-making behaviors. ShuttleEnv serves as a reusable platform for research, visualization, and demonstration of intelligent agents in sports AI. Our ShuttleEnv demo video URL: https://drive.google.com/file/d/1hTR4P16U27H2O0-w316bR73pxE2ucczX/view

</details>


### [169] [A Progressive Visual-Logic-Aligned Framework for Ride-Hailing Adjudication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17328)
*Weiming Wu,Zi-Jian Cheng,Jie Meng,Peng Zhen,Shan Huang,Qun Li,Guobin Wu,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RideJudge，一种渐进式视觉-逻辑对齐框架，用于解决网约车责任纠纷自动化裁决中的语义鸿沟、证据推理和决策透明性问题，通过合成轨迹生成、自适应上下文优化和序数敏感强化学习，在8B模型上实现88.41%的准确率，超越更大规模基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网约车订单激增使得人工审核不可行，传统自动化方法缺乏司法级推理透明度，现有多模态大模型在法律证据严谨性方面存在感知幻觉与逻辑松散问题，难以满足责任判定需求。

Method: 提出RideJudge框架：1）SynTraj合成引擎将抽象责任概念具象化为轨迹模式以弥合语义鸿沟；2）自适应上下文优化策略压缩海量法规知识；3）Chain-of-Adjudication机制实现主动证据推理；4）序数敏感强化学习利用层级化反馈优化决策边界。

Result: RideJudge-8B在实验中达到88.41%的准确率，超过32B规模的基线模型，显著提升裁决的准确性与可解释性。

Conclusion: RideJudge通过视觉-逻辑协同对齐机制，有效解决了多模态大模型在司法场景下的语义不匹配、上下文受限和反馈稀疏问题，为可解释性自动化裁决提供了新范式。

Abstract: The efficient adjudication of responsibility disputes is pivotal for maintaining marketplace fairness. However, the exponential surge in ride-hailing volume renders manual review intractable, while conventional automated methods lack the reasoning transparency required for quasi-judicial decisions. Although Multimodal LLMs offer a promising paradigm, they fundamentally struggle to bridge the gap between general visual semantics and rigorous evidentiary protocols, often leading to perceptual hallucinations and logical looseness. To address these systemic misalignments, we introduce RideJudge, a Progressive Visual-Logic-Aligned Framework. Instead of relying on generic pre-training, we bridge the semantic gap via SynTraj, a synthesis engine that grounds abstract liability concepts into concrete trajectory patterns. To resolve the conflict between massive regulation volume and limited context windows, we propose an Adaptive Context Optimization strategy that distills expert knowledge, coupled with a Chain-of-Adjudication mechanism to enforce active evidentiary inquiry. Furthermore, addressing the inadequacy of sparse binary feedback for complex liability assessment, we implement a novel Ordinal-Sensitive Reinforcement Learning mechanism that calibrates decision boundaries against hierarchical severity. Extensive experiments show that our RideJudge-8B achieves 88.41\% accuracy, surpassing 32B-scale baselines and establishing a new standard for interpretable adjudication.

</details>


### [170] [Towards Safer Large Reasoning Models by Promoting Safety Decision-Making before Chain-of-Thought Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17368)
*Jianan Chen,Zhifang Zhang,Shuo He,Linan Yue,Lei Feng,Minling Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的安全对齐方法，通过在链式思维（CoT）生成前增强大推理模型（LRM）的安全决策能力，有效缓解了CoT启用后带来的安全性能下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现大推理模型在启用链式思维（CoT）后安全能力显著下降，因此需要在CoT生成前加强其安全决策机制。

Method: 利用基于Bert的分类器从安全模型中提取安全决策信号，并将其作为辅助监督信号融入大推理模型的安全对齐过程中，使安全梯度可反向传播至模型的潜在表示。

Result: 实验表明，该方法显著提升了大推理模型的安全能力，同时保持了其原有的推理性能。

Conclusion: 在CoT生成前进行安全决策干预是一种有效提升大推理模型安全性的新思路，具有良好的应用潜力。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) achieved remarkable performance via chain-of-thought (CoT), but recent studies showed that such enhanced reasoning capabilities are at the expense of significantly degraded safety capabilities. In this paper, we reveal that LRMs' safety degradation occurs only after CoT is enabled, and this degradation is not observed when CoT is disabled. This observation motivates us to consider encouraging LRMs to make safety decisions before CoT generation. To this end, we propose a novel safety alignment method that promotes the safety decision-making of LRMs before starting CoT generation. Specifically, we first utilize a Bert-based classifier to extract safety decision signals from a safe model (e.g., a CoT-disabled LRM) and then integrate these signals into LRMs' safety alignment as auxiliary supervision. In this way, the safety gradients can be backpropagated to the LRMs' latent representations, effectively strengthening the LRMs' safety decision-making abilities against CoT generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method substantially improves the safety capabilities of LRMs while effectively maintaining LRMs' general reasoning performance.

</details>


### [171] [Proactive Knowledge Inquiry in Doctor-Patient Dialogue: Stateful Extraction, Belief Updating, and Path-Aware Action Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17425)
*Zhenhai Pan,Yan Liu,Jia You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于部分可观测环境下主动知识探究的医生-患者对话建模框架，通过状态提取、信念更新、缺口感知建模和轻量级POMDP规划器，实现更结构化、低冗余的电子病历生成，在受控模拟实验中表现出良好性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电子病历系统多为事后记录导向，缺乏对已知信息、缺失信息及关键不确定性的建模，无法主动决定下一步应提问或推荐的内容。

Method: 将医患对话建模为部分可观测下的主动知识探究问题，结合有状态信息抽取、序列化信念更新、缺口感知状态建模、混合医学知识检索与轻量级POMDP动作规划器。

Result: 在10个标准化多轮对话和300次检索组成的受控实验中，该框架达到83.3%覆盖率、80.0%风险召回率、81.4%结构完整性，且冗余低于基线方法。

Conclusion: 该框架在受控模拟条件下展示了主动探究策略的可行性与概念优势，值得进一步研究，但尚未证明可用于临床部署。

Abstract: Most automated electronic medical record (EMR) pipelines remain output-oriented: they transcribe, extract, and summarize after the consultation, but they do not explicitly model what is already known, what is still missing, which uncertainty matters most, or what question or recommendation should come next. We formulate doctor-patient dialogue as a proactive knowledge-inquiry problem under partial observability. The proposed framework combines stateful extraction, sequential belief updating, gap-aware state modeling, hybrid retrieval over objectified medical knowledge, and a POMDP-lite action planner. Instead of treating the EMR as the only target artifact, the framework treats documentation as the structured projection of an ongoing inquiry loop. To make the formulation concrete, we report a controlled pilot evaluation on ten standardized multi-turn dialogues together with a 300-query retrieval benchmark aggregated across dialogues. On this pilot protocol, the full framework reaches 83.3% coverage, 80.0% risk recall, 81.4% structural completeness, and lower redundancy than the chunk-only and template-heavy interactive baselines. These pilot results do not establish clinical generalization; rather, they suggest that proactive inquiry may be methodologically interesting under tightly controlled conditions and can be viewed as a conceptually appealing formulation worth further investigation for dialogue-based EMR generation. This work should be read as a pilot concept demonstration under a controlled simulated setting rather than as evidence of clinical deployment readiness. No implication of clinical deployment readiness, clinical safety, or real-world clinical utility should be inferred from this pilot protocol.

</details>


### [172] [When Only the Final Text Survives: Implicit Execution Tracing for Multi-Agent Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17445)
*Yi Nian,Haosen Cao,Shenzhe Zhu,Henry Peng Zou,Qingqing Luan,Yue Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为IET（隐式执行追踪）的元数据无关框架，能够从生成文本中直接实现令牌级归因，并重建多智能体语言系统的交互拓扑结构，支持隐私保护的审计功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当多智能体系统产生错误或有害答案时，若缺乏执行日志和智能体标识，难以确定责任归属。现有方法常掩盖了智能体间的交互结构与贡献，导致问责困难。

Method: 通过在生成过程中将智能体特有的密钥信号嵌入到令牌分布中，使文本自身携带可追溯的执行轨迹；在检测阶段，采用感知状态转移的评分方法识别智能体交接点，进而重构交互图。

Result: 实验表明，IET能以高准确率恢复智能体的贡献片段和协作结构，同时保持生成质量不受影响。

Conclusion: IET为多智能体语言系统提供了一种无需额外元数据、兼顾隐私保护与可审计性的新型追踪机制。

Abstract: When a multi-agent system produces an incorrect or harmful answer, who is accountable if execution logs and agent identifiers are unavailable? Multi-agent language systems increasingly rely on structured interactions such as delegation and iterative refinement, yet the final output often obscures the underlying interaction topology and agent contributions. We introduce IET (Implicit Execution Tracing), a metadata-independent framework that enables token-level attribution directly from generated text and a simple mechanism for interaction topology reconstruction. During generation, agent-specific keyed signals are embedded into the token distribution, transforming the text into a self-describing execution trace detectable only with a secret key. At detection time, a transition-aware scoring method identifies agent handover points and reconstructs the interaction graph. Experiments show that IET recovers agent segments and coordination structure with high accuracy while preserving generation quality, enabling privacy-preserving auditing for multi-agent language systems.

</details>


### [173] [Informative Semi-Factuals for XAI: The Elaborated Explanations that People Prefer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17534)
*Saugat Aryal,Mark T. Keane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的可解释人工智能方法——信息性半事实解释（ISF），通过补充影响决策的隐藏特征信息，生成更丰富、高质量的半事实解释，并在实验和用户研究中验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的半事实解释方法仅说明某些输入变化不会改变结果，但未解释为何如此；本文旨在提供更具信息性的解释，揭示关键隐藏特征对决策的影响机制。

Method: 提出信息性半事实解释（ISF）算法，在传统半事实基础上识别并整合影响模型输出的隐藏特征，生成更详尽的解释。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，ISF生成的解释在关键指标上具有高质量；用户研究显示，用户更偏好这种增强型解释。

Conclusion: ISF方法提升了半事实解释的信息量与可理解性，有助于增强用户对AI决策的信任与理解。

Abstract: Recently, in eXplainable AI (XAI), $\textit{even if}$ explanations -- so-called semi-factuals -- have emerged as a popular strategy that explains how a predicted outcome $\textit{can remain the same}$ even when certain input-features are altered. For example, in the commonly-used banking app scenario, a semi-factual explanation could inform customers about better options, other alternatives for their successful application, by saying "$\textit{Even if}$ you asked for double the loan amount, you would still be accepted". Most semi-factuals XAI algorithms focus on finding maximal value-changes to a single key-feature that do $\textit{not}$ alter the outcome (unlike counterfactual explanations that often find minimal value-changes to several features that alter the outcome). However, no current semi-factual method explains $\textit{why}$ these extreme value-changes do not alter outcomes; for example, a more informative semi-factual could tell the customer that it is their good credit score that allows them to borrow double their requested loan. In this work, we advance a new algorithm -- the $\textit{informative semi-factuals}$ (ISF) method -- that generates more elaborated explanations supplementing semi-factuals with information about additional $\textit{hidden features}$ that influence an automated decision. Experimental results on benchmark datasets show that this ISF method computes semi-factuals that are both informative and of high-quality on key metrics. Furthermore, a user study shows that people prefer these elaborated explanations over the simpler semi-factual explanations generated by current methods.

</details>


### [174] [VeriGrey: Greybox Agent Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17639)
*Yuntong Zhang,Sungmin Kang,Ruijie Meng,Marcel Böhme,Abhik Roychoudhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种灰盒测试方法VeriGrey，用于发现大语言模型（LLM）智能体中的安全风险，特别是间接提示注入漏洞。该方法通过工具调用序列作为反馈来指导测试过程，并通过变异提示生成恶意注入提示，在多个真实场景中表现出优于黑盒方法的检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体通过结合外部工具实现自主决策，但其与环境的交互带来了新的安全威胁，尤其是提示注入等攻击。现有黑盒测试方法难以有效发现这些隐蔽且危险的行为，因此需要更高效的测试机制。

Method: 提出VeriGrey，一种基于灰盒的测试框架：利用智能体执行过程中调用的工具序列为反馈信号，驱动测试流程；采用突变操作对提示进行变异，构造具有攻击性的注入提示；将注入任务与智能体原任务关联，使攻击成为完成任务的必要步骤，从而触发潜在漏洞。

Result: 在AgentDojo基准上，VeriGrey比黑盒基线多发现33%的间接提示注入漏洞（GPT-4.1后端）；在Gemini CLI和OpenClaw等真实系统中成功发现多种新型攻击场景；在OpenClaw中以100%（Kimi-K2.5）和90%（Opus 4.6）的成功率从10个恶意技能中发现了恶意变体。

Conclusion: VeriGrey展示了灰盒测试在LLM智能体安全评估中的有效性，其动态反馈机制显著提升了对隐蔽漏洞的检测能力，为构建智能体安全保障框架提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Agentic AI has been a topic of great interest recently. A Large Language Model (LLM) agent involves one or more LLMs in the back-end. In the front end, it conducts autonomous decision-making by combining the LLM outputs with results obtained by invoking several external tools. The autonomous interactions with the external environment introduce critical security risks.
  In this paper, we present a grey-box approach to explore diverse behaviors and uncover security risks in LLM agents. Our approach VeriGrey uses the sequence of tools invoked as a feedback function to drive the testing process. This helps uncover infrequent but dangerous tool invocations that cause unexpected agent behavior. As mutation operators in the testing process, we mutate prompts to design pernicious injection prompts. This is carefully accomplished by linking the task of the agent to an injection task, so that the injection task becomes a necessary step of completing the agent functionality. Comparing our approach with a black-box baseline on the well-known AgentDojo benchmark, VeriGrey achieves 33% additional efficacy in finding indirect prompt injection vulnerabilities with a GPT-4.1 back-end.
  We also conduct real-world case studies with the widely used coding agent Gemini CLI, and the well-known OpenClaw personal assistant. VeriGrey finds prompts inducing several attack scenarios that could not be identified by black-box approaches. In OpenClaw, by constructing a conversation agent which employs mutational fuzz testing as needed, VeriGrey is able to discover malicious skill variants from 10 malicious skills (with 10/10= 100% success rate on the Kimi-K2.5 LLM backend, and 9/10= 90% success rate on Opus 4.6 LLM backend). This demonstrates the value of a dynamic approach like VeriGrey to test agents, and to eventually lead to an agent assurance framework.

</details>


### [175] [MALLES: A Multi-agent LLMs-based Economic Sandbox with Consumer Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17694)
*Yusen Wu,Yiran Liu,Xiaotie Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多智能体大语言模型的经济沙盘（MALLES），通过偏好学习和均场机制，在高维多模态环境中实现跨领域、跨品类的高保真经济决策模拟，有效缓解数据稀疏问题并提升模拟稳定性与预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实经济中的决策面临高维、多模态环境、智能体异质性和组合性数据稀疏等挑战，传统方法难以实现跨域泛化与稳定模拟，因此需要一种更具扩展性和适应性的统一框架。

Method: 提出MALLES框架：1）通过在多样化产品类别的交易记录上对LLM进行后训练，实现经济对齐的偏好学习；2）引入均场机制建模产品环境与用户群体的动态交互，稳定高维空间采样；3）设计多智能体对话框架，通过协作处理产品信息分担认知负荷，利用结构化对话捕捉关键决策因素。

Result: 实验表明，该框架在产品选择准确率、购买数量预测和模拟稳定性方面显著优于现有经济与金融LLM模拟基线方法。

Conclusion: 大语言模型可作为现实经济中高保真、可扩展决策模拟的基础工具，结合偏好学习、均场机制与多智能体协作，为复杂经济系统建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: In the real economy, modern decision-making is fundamentally challenged by high-dimensional, multimodal environments, which are further complicated by agent heterogeneity and combinatorial data sparsity. This paper introduces a Multi-Agent Large Language Model-based Economic Sandbox (MALLES), leveraging the inherent generalization capabilities of large-sacle models to establish a unified simulation framework applicable to cross-domain and cross-category scenarios. Central to our approach is a preference learning paradigm in which LLMs are economically aligned via post-training on extensive, heterogeneous transaction records across diverse product categories. This methodology enables the models to internalize and transfer latent consumer preference patterns, thereby mitigating the data sparsity issues prevalent in individual categories. To enhance simulation stability, we implement a mean-field mechanism designed to model the dynamic interactions between the product environment and customer populations, effectively stabilizing sampling processes within high-dimensional decision spaces. Furthermore, we propose a multi-agent discussion framework wherein specialized agents collaboratively process extensive product information. This architecture distributes cognitive load to alleviate single-agent attention bottlenecks and captures critical decision factors through structured dialogue. Experiments demonstrate that our framework achieves significant improvements in product selection accuracy, purchase quantity prediction, and simulation stability compared to existing economic and financial LLM simulation baselines. Our results substantiate the potential of large language models as a foundational pillar for high-fidelity, scalable decision simulation and latter analysis in the real economy based on foundational database.

</details>


### [176] [From Virtual Environments to Real-World Trials: Emerging Trends in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17714)
*A. Humnabadkar,A. Sikdar,B. Cave,H. Zhang,N. Bessis,A. Behera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文综述了合成数据和虚拟环境在自动驾驶技术中的关键作用，涵盖感知与规划、数字孪生仿真验证及域适应策略，并探讨了通向安全、可泛化自动驾驶系统的关键挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于真实世界数据稀缺、安全性要求高以及跨环境泛化能力不足，自动驾驶的实际部署面临挑战，因此需要利用合成数据和仿真环境来弥补现实数据的不足并提升系统鲁棒性。

Method: 通过系统性综述的方法，从三个维度梳理研究进展：合成数据在感知与规划中的应用、基于数字孪生的系统验证、连接合成与真实世界的域适应策略，并构建数据集、工具与仿真平台的分类体系。

Result: 提供了自动驾驶中合成数据与仿真的全面综述，提出了涵盖数据、模型与评估的 taxonomy，揭示了当前在 Sim2Real 转移、安全验证、协同自主和仿真驱动策略学习等方面的技术趋势与瓶颈。

Conclusion: 合成数据与高保真仿真是推动自动驾驶研发与验证的关键手段，未来需进一步提升仿真真实性、发展有效的域自适应方法，并构建标准化基准以支持安全可靠的全球部署。

Abstract: Autonomous driving technologies have achieved significant advances in recent years, yet their real-world deployment remains constrained by data scarcity, safety requirements, and the need for generalization across diverse environments. In response, synthetic data and virtual environments have emerged as powerful enablers, offering scalable, controllable, and richly annotated scenarios for training and evaluation. This survey presents a comprehensive review of recent developments at the intersection of autonomous driving, simulation technologies, and synthetic datasets. We organize the landscape across three core dimensions: (i) the use of synthetic data for perception and planning, (ii) digital twin-based simulation for system validation, and (iii) domain adaptation strategies bridging synthetic and real-world data. We also highlight the role of vision-language models and simulation realism in enhancing scene understanding and generalization. A detailed taxonomy of datasets, tools, and simulation platforms is provided, alongside an analysis of trends in benchmark design. Finally, we discuss critical challenges and open research directions, including Sim2Real transfer, scalable safety validation, cooperative autonomy, and simulation-driven policy learning, that must be addressed to accelerate the path toward safe, generalizable, and globally deployable autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [177] [Facts as First Class Objects: Knowledge Objects for Persistent LLM Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17781)
*Oliver Zahn,Simran Chana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较了大语言模型中上下文记忆与知识对象（KOs）的性能，发现KOs在准确性、成本和多跳推理上显著优于传统方法，并揭示了现有架构在记忆压缩和检索上的系统性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在解决大语言模型作为持久化知识工作者时，依赖上下文记忆所导致的容量限制、信息丢失和目标偏移等问题，探索更高效可靠的记忆机制。

Method: 通过构建基准测试集，对比分析上下文记忆与离散哈希寻址的知识对象（KOs）在不同条件下的表现，并在四个前沿模型上进行跨模型复现，验证结果的普适性。同时引入密度自适应检索作为切换机制。

Result: 实验显示，在上下文窗口内，Claude Sonnet 4.5对10到7,000个事实的精确匹配准确率达100%，但生产环境中出现提示溢出、压缩损失60%事实、项目约束衰减54%等故障；KOs在所有条件下保持100%准确率，成本降低252倍；多跳推理准确率KOs为78.9%，远高于上下文的31.6%；嵌入式检索在对抗性事实上精度仅为20%（top-1），神经记忆能存储但无法按需提取。

Conclusion: 知识对象（KOs）是一种更优的记忆架构，解决了上下文记忆的系统性缺陷，具有高精度、低成本和强鲁棒性，适合大规模部署；研究呼吁重新思考模型记忆机制的设计范式。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly serve as persistent knowledge workers, with in-context memory - facts stored in the prompt - as the default strategy. We benchmark in-context memory against Knowledge Objects (KOs), discrete hash-addressed tuples with O(1) retrieval. Within the context window, Claude Sonnet 4.5 achieves 100% exact-match accuracy from 10 to 7,000 facts (97.5% of its 200K window). However, production deployment reveals three failure modes: capacity limits (prompts overflow at 8,000 facts), compaction loss (summarization destroys 60% of facts), and goal drift (cascading compaction erodes 54% of project constraints while the model continues with full confidence). KOs achieve 100% accuracy across all conditions at 252x lower cost. On multi-hop reasoning, KOs reach 78.9% versus 31.6% for in-context. Cross-model replication across four frontier models confirms compaction loss is architectural, not model-specific. We additionally show that embedding retrieval fails on adversarial facts (20% precision at 1) and that neural memory (Titans) stores facts but fails to retrieve them on demand. We introduce density-adaptive retrieval as a switching mechanism and release the benchmark suite.

</details>


### [178] [RPMS: Enhancing LLM-Based Embodied Planning through Rule-Augmented Memory Synergy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17831)
*Zhenhang Yuan,Shenghai Yuan,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为RPMS的冲突管理架构，通过结构化规则检索、轻量级信念状态和规则优先仲裁机制，有效解决了LLM智能体在封闭世界具身环境中因动作无效生成和状态漂移导致的性能下降问题，在ALFWorld和ScienceWorld任务中显著提升了成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在封闭世界具身环境中常因动作需满足严格前提条件且失败反馈稀疏而表现不佳，存在无效动作生成与状态漂移两种相互加剧的失败模式，亟需一种能协调规则与记忆冲突的架构来提升鲁棒性。

Method: 提出RPMS架构：1）通过结构化规则检索确保动作可行性；2）引入轻量级信念状态控制记忆适用性；3）采用规则优先的仲裁机制解决规则与记忆间的冲突。

Result: 在ALFWorld（134个未见任务）上，RPMS使用Llama 3.1 8B实现59.7%单次试验成功率（比基线高23.9个百分点），其中规则检索单独贡献+14.9个百分点；使用Claude Sonnet 4.5达到98.5%成功率（+11.9 pp）。在ScienceWorld上适配GPT-4后，平均得分从44.9提升至54.0，所有消融条件下均一致增益。

Conclusion: 规则检索是提升性能的主导因素；情景记忆仅在被当前状态过滤并受显式规则约束时才成为稳定正向因素；RPMS的核心机制可在结构不同的环境中迁移，验证了其通用有效性。

Abstract: LLM agents often fail in closed-world embodied environments because actions must satisfy strict preconditions -- such as location, inventory, and container states -- and failure feedback is sparse. We identify two structurally coupled failure modes: (P1) invalid action generation and (P2) state drift, each amplifying the other in a degenerative cycle. We present RPMS, a conflict-managed architecture that enforces action feasibility via structured rule retrieval, gates memory applicability via a lightweight belief state, and resolves conflicts between the two sources via rules-first arbitration. On ALFWorld (134 unseen tasks), RPMS achieves 59.7% single-trial success with Llama 3.1 8B (+23.9 pp over baseline) and 98.5% with Claude Sonnet 4.5 (+11.9 pp); of the 8B gain, rule retrieval alone contributes +14.9 pp (statistically significant), making it the dominant factor. A key finding is that episodic memory is conditionally useful: it harms performance on some task types when used without grounding, but becomes a stable net positive once filtered by current state and constrained by explicit action rules. Adapting RPMS to ScienceWorld with GPT-4 yields consistent gains across all ablation conditions (avg. score 54.0 vs. 44.9 for the ReAct baseline), providing transfer evidence that the core mechanisms hold across structurally distinct environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Embodied Foundation Models at the Edge: A Survey of Deployment Constraints and Mitigation Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16952)
*Utkarsh Grover,Ravi Ranjan,Mingyang Mao,Trung Tien Dong,Satvik Praveen,Zhenqi Wu,J. Morris Chang,Tinoosh Mohsenin,Yi Sheng,Agoritsa Polyzou,Eiman Kanjo,Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文讨论了在具身边缘系统中部署基础模型的系统性挑战，提出了“部署八重障碍”框架，并指出不同类型的模型（如自回归与扩散模型）面临的主要限制，强调系统级协同设计的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决基础模型在边缘设备上实时控制中的部署难题，超越单纯的模型压缩，关注系统层面的综合优化。

Method: 提出“Deployment Gauntlet”框架，归纳出八个相互关联的部署障碍，并通过代表性边缘工作负载分析自回归和扩散模型的性能瓶颈。

Result: 发现自回归视觉-语言-动作策略主要受限于内存带宽，而扩散控制器则更受计算延迟和持续执行成本限制。

Conclusion: 可靠部署依赖于内存、调度、通信和模型架构的系统级协同设计，包括将快速控制与慢速语义推理分离的模型分解方法。

Abstract: Deploying foundation models in embodied edge systems is fundamentally a systems problem, not just a problem of model compression. Real-time control must operate within strict size, weight, and power constraints, where memory traffic, compute latency, timing variability, and safety margins interact directly. The Deployment Gauntlet organizes these constraints into eight coupled barriers that determine whether embodied foundation models can run reliably in practice. Across representative edge workloads, autoregressive Vision-Language-Action policies are constrained primarily by memory bandwidth, whereas diffusion-based controllers are limited more by compute latency and sustained execution cost. Reliable deployment therefore depends on system-level co-design across memory, scheduling, communication, and model architecture, including decompositions that separate fast control from slower semantic reasoning.

</details>


### [180] [Rewarding DINO: Predicting Dense Rewards with Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16978)
*Pierre Krack,Tobias Jülg,Wolfram Burgard,Florian Walter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Rewarding DINO的语言条件奖励建模方法，通过从视觉数据中学习真实奖励函数而非特定轨迹，实现了在仿真和现实环境中对机器人操作任务的有效泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的密集奖励函数设计依赖于模拟环境中的特权状态信息，难以在真实世界中应用；基于专家演示的方法容易偏向特定解决方案且覆盖不全。因此需要一种能从视觉输入中学习通用奖励函数的方法。

Method: 提出Rewarding DINO，利用语言条件和DINO模型结构，基于排序损失（rank-based loss）在Meta-World+任务的数据上训练奖励预测模型，并使用视觉输入直接预测奖励值。

Result: Rewarding DINO在训练任务中表现出竞争性的性能，在配对准确率、秩相关性和校准方面表现良好，并能在未见场景中泛化到仿真和真实世界设置，验证了其对任务语义的学习能力。结合现成的强化学习算法可成功解决训练集任务。

Conclusion: Rewarding DINO能够有效学习与任务语义一致的密集奖励函数，具备良好的泛化能力和低计算开销，可作为解析奖励函数的替代方案应用于真实机器人操作任务。

Abstract: Well-designed dense reward functions in robot manipulation not only indicate whether a task is completed but also encode progress along the way. Generally, designing dense rewards is challenging and usually requires access to privileged state information available only in simulation, not in real-world experiments. This makes reward prediction models that infer task state information from camera images attractive. A common approach is to predict rewards from expert demonstrations based on visual similarity or sequential frame ordering. However, this biases the resulting reward function towards a specific solution and leaves it undefined in states not covered by the demonstrations. In this work, we introduce Rewarding DINO, a method for language-conditioned reward modeling that learns actual reward functions rather than specific trajectories. The model's compact size allows it to serve as a direct replacement for analytical reward functions with comparatively low computational overhead. We train our model on data sampled from 24 Meta-World+ tasks using a rank-based loss and evaluate pairwise accuracy, rank correlation, and calibration. Rewarding DINO achieves competitive performance in tasks from the training set and generalizes to new settings in simulation and the real world, indicating that it learns task semantics. We also test the model with off-the-shelf reinforcement learning algorithms to solve tasks from our Meta-World+ training set.

</details>


### [181] [Contingency-Aware Planning via Certified Neural Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17022)
*Kasidit Muenprasitivej,Derya Aksaray*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合学习型可达性与采样规划的应急感知多目标导航框架，通过FNO近似高维HJ方程解算器，并提供安全性的形式化验证，实现实时最优导航与应急行为保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HJ可达性方法因计算高维偏微分方程而难以用于实时规划，需在未知环境中实现高效且安全的多目标导航。

Method: 采用傅里叶神经算子（FNO）逼近不同障碍物配置下的Hamilton-Jacobi-Isaacs变分不等式的解算器，结合增量式多目标规划器，引入可达集约束和可保证有限时间返回安全区域的恢复策略。

Result: 实现了对后向可达-避免集的理论下界估计，提供了学习可达集的形式化安全认证，并在Webots仿真中于KUKA youBot平台实现实时部署。

Conclusion: 所提框架在未知环境中实现了渐近最优导航与可证明的应急响应能力，兼顾安全性与实时性。

Abstract: Hamilton-Jacobi (HJ) reachability provides formal safety guarantees for dynamical systems, but solving high-dimensional HJ partial differential equations limits its use in real-time planning. This paper presents a contingency-aware multi-goal navigation framework that integrates learning-based reachability with sampling-based planning in unknown environments. We use Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) to approximate the solution operator of the Hamilton-Jacobi-Isaacs variational inequality under varying obstacle configurations. We first provide a theoretical under-approximation guarantee on the safe backward reach-avoid set, which enables formal safety certification of the learned reachable sets. Then, we integrate the certified reachable sets with an incremental multi-goal planner, which enforces reachable-set constraints and a recovery policy that guarantees finite-time return to a safe region. Overall, we demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves asymptotically optimal navigation with provable contingency behavior, and validate its performance through real-time deployment on KUKA's youBot in Webots simulation.

</details>


### [182] [TeleDex: Accessible Dexterous Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17065)
*Omar Rayyan,Maximilian Gillesm,Yuchen Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TeleDex是一个开源系统，利用普通手机实现灵巧手和机械臂的直观遥操作，支持低延迟、无需外部追踪设备的6-DoF腕部姿态和21-DoF手势估计，降低硬件门槛，便于快速收集演示数据用于策略微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作策略在新环境、任务或机器人形态中泛化能力差，部署时需额外收集演示数据，但传统方法成本高、设置复杂，亟需一种便捷、低成本的遥操作工具。

Method: 开发TeleDex系统，通过手机实时流式传输6-DoF手腕姿态和21-DoF手部状态，并将其重定向至机器人；支持手持模式和可选3D打印手装接口，实现手指级控制，无需外部追踪基础设施。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界操作任务中验证了系统的有效性，证明其可作为统一且可扩展的机器人遥操作接口，显著降低硬件与设置门槛。

Conclusion: TeleDex通过利用普及型手机实现了低成本、易部署的灵巧遥操作，为机器人策略在实际部署中的快速演示收集和微调提供了可行方案，推动了遥操作技术的普及化和可扩展性。

Abstract: Despite increasing dataset scale and model capacity, robot manipulation policies still struggle to generalize beyond their training distributions. As a result, deploying state-of-the-art policies in new environments, tasks, or robot embodiments often requires collecting additional demonstrations. Enabling this in real-world deployment settings requires tools that allow users to collect demonstrations quickly, affordably, and with minimal setup. We present TeleDex, an open-source system for intuitive teleoperation of dexterous hands and robotic manipulators using any readily available phone. The system streams low-latency 6-DoF wrist poses and articulated 21-DoF hand state estimates from the phone, which are retargeted to robot arms and multi-fingered hands without requiring external tracking infrastructure. TeleDex supports both a handheld phone-only mode and an optional 3D-printable hand-mounted interface for finger-level teleoperation. By lowering the hardware and setup barriers to dexterous teleoperation, TeleDex enables users to quickly collect demonstrations during deployment to support policy fine-tuning. We evaluate the system across simulation and real-world manipulation tasks, demonstrating its effectiveness as a unified scalable interface for robot teleoperation. All software and hardware designs, along with demonstration videos, are open-source and available at orayyan.com/teledex.

</details>


### [183] [SLowRL: Safe Low-Rank Adaptation Reinforcement Learning for Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17092)
*Elham Daneshmand,Shafeef Omar,Glen Berseth,Majid Khadiv,Hsiu-Chin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SLowRL的框架，用于在真实硬件上安全高效地微调动态运动任务中的强化学习策略。该方法结合了低秩适应（LoRA）和训练时通过恢复策略实施的安全约束，在模拟和真实的四足机器人实验中显著减少了微调时间并几乎消除了安全违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于仿真与现实之间的差距，将仿真中学习到的运动策略直接迁移到现实中常导致性能下降。直接在硬件上微调存在机械故障风险且样本效率低，因此需要一种既能保证安全又能提高效率的微调方法。

Method: 提出SLowRL框架，结合低秩适应（LoRA）与基于恢复策略的训练时安全约束机制，在不牺牲性能的前提下实现对预训练策略的安全微调。

Result: 在模拟和真实Unitree Go2四足机器人上的跳躍和小跑任务中，相比标准PPO基线，微调时间减少46.5%，安全违规接近零；仅需秩为1的适应即可恢复真实环境下的预训练性能。

Conclusion: SLowRL实现了安全、高效的现实世界动态运动策略微调，验证了其在实际机器人应用中的可行性与实用性。

Abstract: Sim-to-real transfer of locomotion policies often leads to performance degradation due to the inevitable sim-to-real gap. Naively fine-tuning these policies directly on hardware is problematic, as it poses risks of mechanical failure and suffers from high sample inefficiency. In this paper, we address the challenge of safely and efficiently fine-tuning reinforcement learning (RL) policies for dynamic locomotion tasks. Specifically, we focus on fine-tuning policies learned in simulation directly on hardware, while explicitly enforcing safety constraints. In doing so, we introduce SLowRL, a framework that combines Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) with training-time safety enforcement via a recovery policy. We evaluate our method both in simulation and on a real Unitree Go2 quadruped robot for jump and trot tasks. Experimental results show that our method achieves a $46.5\%$ reduction in fine-tuning time and near-zero safety violations compared to standard proximal policy optimization (PPO) baselines. Notably, we find that a rank-1 adaptation alone is sufficient to recover pre-trained performance in the real world, while maintaining stable and safe real-world fine-tuning. These results demonstrate the practicality of safe, efficient fine-tuning for dynamic real-world robotic applications.

</details>


### [184] [Shielded Reinforcement Learning Under Dynamic Temporal Logic Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17152)
*Sadık Bera Yüksel,Ali Tevfik Buyukkocak,Derya Aksaray*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合信号时序逻辑（STL）和无模型强化学习的框架，通过序列控制屏障函数确保在学习过程中满足复杂的时空任务约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习在真实系统中部署受限于安全和操作约束，而传统安全RL方法难以处理如周期充电或时间限制访问等复杂时空任务。

Method: 利用信号时态逻辑（STL）描述复杂任务，并结合序列控制屏障函数与无模型强化学习，在学习过程中动态保证任务约束的满足。

Result: 该方法能够有效处理涉及动态目标（轨迹未知）访问的丰富STL规范，并在多种仿真环境中验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 所提框架成功将STL任务集成到强化学习中，实现了在学习全过程中的复杂时空约束满足，扩展了安全强化学习的能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has shown promise in various robotics applications, yet its deployment on real systems is still limited due to safety and operational constraints. The safe RL field has gained considerable attention in recent years, which focuses on imposing safety constraints throughout the learning process. However, real systems often require more complex constraints than just safety, such as periodic recharging or time-bounded visits to specific regions. Imposing such spatio-temporal tasks during learning still remains a challenge. Signal Temporal Logic (STL) is a formal language for specifying temporal properties of real-valued signals and provides a way to express such complex tasks. In this paper, we propose a framework that leverages sequential control barrier functions and model-free RL to ensure that the given STL tasks are satisfied throughout the learning process. Our method extends beyond traditional safety constraints by enforcing rich STL specifications, which can involve visits to dynamic targets with unknown trajectories. We also demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework through various simulations.

</details>


### [185] [SLAM Adversarial Lab: An Extensible Framework for Visual SLAM Robustness Evaluation under Adverse Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17165)
*Mohamed Hefny,Karthik Dantu,Steven Y. Ko*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAL是一个模块化框架，用于在雾、雨等对抗性条件下评估视觉SLAM系统，支持可解释的严重程度级别和可扩展架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了评估视觉SLAM系统在真实世界对抗性天气条件下的鲁棒性，需要一个统一且可扩展的评估框架。

Method: SAL将每种对抗性条件建模为数据集的扰动，使用现实单位（如米）表示严重程度，并通过解耦设计支持多种数据集、扰动和SLAM算法的集成。

Result: 框架成功集成了七种SLAM算法和三个数据集，在天气、相机和视频传输扰动下进行了评估，并能自动搜索导致SLAM系统失效的扰动严重程度。

Conclusion: SAL提供了一个灵活、可扩展且易于使用的平台，用于系统性地评估视觉SLAM在对抗性条件下的性能。

Abstract: We present SAL (SLAM Adversarial Lab), a modular framework for evaluating visual SLAM systems under adversarial conditions such as fog and rain. SAL represents each adversarial condition as a perturbation that transforms an existing dataset into an adversarial dataset. When transforming a dataset, SAL supports severity levels using easily-interpretable real-world units such as meters for fog visibility. SAL's extensible architecture decouples datasets, perturbations, and SLAM algorithms through common interfaces, so users can add new components without rewriting integration code. Moreover, SAL includes a search procedure that finds the severity level of a perturbation at which a SLAM system fails. To showcase the capabilities of SAL, our evaluation integrates seven SLAM algorithms and evaluates them across three datasets under weather, camera, and video transport perturbations.

</details>


### [186] [Influence of Gripper Design on Human Demonstration Quality for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17189)
*Gina L. Georgadarellis,Natalija Beslic,Seonhun Lee,Frank C. Sup,Meghan E. Huber*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究评估了通用操作界面（UMI）手持抓取工具在示范开启绷带包装任务中的有效性，比较了三种条件：分布负载抓取器、集中负载抓取器和徒手操作。结果表明，集中负载抓取器表现优于分布负载，但仍显著逊色于徒手操作，凸显了对手持抓取器进行人机工程学和机械改进的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升机器人学习医疗操作技能的效率，需优化人类示范所用的手持抓取工具，以应对医疗环境中常见但对机器人具挑战性的无菌包装开启任务。

Method: 通过让八名参与者在三种条件下（分布负载抓取器、集中负载抓取器、徒手）完成计时的开启绷带包装任务，评估任务成功率、完成时间、损坏情况以及使用NASA-TLX问卷测量的感知工作负荷。

Result: 集中负载抓取器在性能上优于分布负载抓取器，但两者均显著慢于且不如徒手操作有效；徒手操作在成功率、速度和损伤控制方面表现最佳。

Conclusion: 当前手持抓取工具在模拟精细医疗操作方面仍存在局限，需进一步进行人机工程学和机械设计优化，以降低用户负担并提高示范质量，推动医疗机器人发展。

Abstract: Opening sterile medical packaging is routine for healthcare workers but remains challenging for robots. Learning from demonstration enables robots to acquire manipulation skills directly from humans, and handheld gripper tools such as the Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) offer a pathway for efficient data collection. However, the effectiveness of these tools depends heavily on their usability. We evaluated UMI in demonstrating a bandage opening task, a common manipulation task in hospital settings, by testing three conditions: distributed load grippers, concentrated load grippers, and bare hands. Eight participants performed timed trials, with task performance assessed by success rate, completion time, and damage, alongside perceived workload using the NASA-TLX questionnaire. Concentrated load grippers improved performance relative to distributed load grippers but remained substantially slower and less effective than hands. These results underscore the importance of ergonomic and mechanical refinements in handheld grippers to reduce user burden and improve demonstration quality, especially for applications in healthcare robotics.

</details>


### [187] [FastLoop: Parallel Loop Closing with GPU-Acceleration in Visual SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17201)
*Soudabeh Mohammadhashemi,Shishir Gopinath,Kimia Khabiri,Parsa Hosseininejad,Karthik Dantu,Steven Y. Ko*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为FastLoop的GPU加速回环检测模块，用于提升视觉SLAM系统的回环检测效率，基于ORB-SLAM3框架并利用CUDA实现，在多个数据集上实现了显著的速度提升，同时保持原有精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回环检测在视觉SLAM中计算开销大，需在整个地图中搜索匹配，限制了系统实时性，因此需要高效加速方法。

Method: 通过分析回环检测流程中的性能瓶颈，采用任务级和数据级并行优化策略，并集成GPU加速的位姿图优化，在ORB-SLAM3基础上使用CUDA实现GPU并行计算。

Result: 在EuRoC数据集上桌面和嵌入式平台平均加速1.4x和1.3x，在TUM-VI数据集上达到3.0x和2.4x的加速效果。

Conclusion: FastLoop有效降低了视觉SLAM中回环检测的计算复杂度，显著提升了处理速度，适用于对实时性要求较高的应用场景。

Abstract: Visual SLAM systems combine visual tracking with global loop closure to maintain a consistent map and accurate localization. Loop closure is a computationally expensive process as we need to search across the whole map for matches. This paper presents FastLoop, a GPU-accelerated loop closing module to alleviate this computational complexity. We identify key performance bottlenecks in the loop closing pipeline of visual SLAM and address them through parallel optimizations on the GPU. Specifically, we use task-level and data-level parallelism and integrate a GPU-accelerated pose graph optimization. Our implementation is built on top of ORB-SLAM3 and leverages CUDA for GPU programming. Experimental results show that FastLoop achieves an average speedup of 1.4x and 1.3x on the EuRoC dataset and 3.0x and 2.4x on the TUM-VI dataset for the loop closing module on desktop and embedded platforms, respectively, while maintaining the accuracy of the original system.

</details>


### [188] [Visual SLAM with DEM Anchoring for Lunar Surface Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17229)
*Adam Dai,Guillem Casadesus Vila,Grace Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合学习特征匹配和数字高程模型（DEM）约束的立体视觉SLAM系统，用于提升月球漫游车在无GPS、极端光照和低纹理地形下的长距离自主导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球缺乏全球定位系统、存在极端光照条件和低纹理月壤，导致传统视觉惯性里程计在长距离行驶中累积漂移，难以实现精确的定位与建图。

Method: 设计了一个立体视觉SLAM系统，前端采用基于学习的特征提取与匹配以增强对光照变化和重复地形的鲁棒性；后端在位姿图中引入由DEM生成的高度和表面法向约束，提供绝对的地表信息以抑制长期漂移。

Result: 在Unreal Engine生成的模拟月球数据和意大利埃特纳火山的真实类月/类火星数据上进行了验证，结果表明，相比基线SLAM方法，引入DEM锚定显著降低了绝对轨迹误差，尤其在重复或视觉混淆地形中仍能有效抑制漂移。

Conclusion: 通过融合学习型特征与DEM提供的先验地形约束，该SLAM系统能够有效提升月球漫游车在复杂环境下的长距离定位精度与地图一致性，具备应用于未来月球探测任务的潜力。

Abstract: Future lunar missions will require autonomous rovers capable of traversing tens of kilometers across challenging terrain while maintaining accurate localization and producing globally consistent maps. However, the absence of global positioning systems, extreme illumination, and low-texture regolith make long-range navigation on the Moon particularly difficult, as visual-inertial odometry pipelines accumulate drift over extended traverses. To address this challenge, we present a stereo visual simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) system that integrates learned feature detection and matching with global constraints from digital elevation models (DEMs). Our front-end employs learning-based feature extraction and matching to achieve robustness to illumination extremes and repetitive terrain, while the back-end incorporates DEM-derived height and surface-normal factors into a pose graph, providing absolute surface constraints that mitigate long-term drift. We validate our approach using both simulated lunar traverse data generated in Unreal Engine and real Moon/Mars analog data collected from Mt. Etna. Results demonstrate that DEM anchoring consistently reduces absolute trajectory error compared to baseline SLAM methods, lowering drift in long-range navigation even in repetitive or visually aliased terrain.

</details>


### [189] [Full Stack Navigation, Mapping, and Planning for the Lunar Autonomy Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17232)
*Adam Dai,Asta Wu,Keidai Iiyama,Guillem Casadesus Vila,Kaila Coimbra,Thomas Deng,Grace Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于月球表面导航与建图的模块化全栈自主系统，在无GNSS且视觉条件恶劣的环境中实现了厘米级定位精度和高保真地图生成，并在Lunar Autonomy Challenge中获得第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏GNSS信号且视觉环境具有挑战性的月球表面，实现高精度、可重复的自主导航与地图构建是一项关键挑战，需要鲁棒的感知、定位与规划控制集成方案。

Method: 采用语义分割、双目视觉里程计、带有回环检测的位姿图SLAM，结合轻量级学习模型进行实时感知，使用因子图后端保证全局一致性；高层规划促进地图覆盖并鼓励频繁回环，局部运动规划通过弧采样和几何避障实现高效反应式控制。

Result: 在高保真月球模拟器中验证了方法的有效性，实现了厘米级定位精度、高质量地图生成，并在不同随机种子和岩石分布下表现出强可重复性。

Conclusion: 所提出的模块化全栈系统在真实感模拟环境中表现优异，成功解决了月面自主导航的关键挑战，具备实际部署潜力。

Abstract: We present a modular, full-stack autonomy system for lunar surface navigation and mapping developed for the Lunar Autonomy Challenge. Operating in a GNSS-denied, visually challenging environment, our pipeline integrates semantic segmentation, stereo visual odometry, pose graph SLAM with loop closures, and layered planning and control. We leverage lightweight learning-based perception models for real-time segmentation and feature tracking and use a factor-graph backend to maintain globally consistent localization. High-level waypoint planning is designed to promote mapping coverage while encouraging frequent loop closures, and local motion planning uses arc sampling with geometric obstacle checks for efficient, reactive control. We evaluate our approach in the competition's high-fidelity lunar simulator, demonstrating centimeter-level localization accuracy, high-fidelity map generation, and strong repeatability across random seeds and rock distributions. Our solution achieved first place in the final competition evaluation.

</details>


### [190] [Neural Radiance Maps for Extraterrestrial Navigation and Path Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17236)
*Adam Dai,Shubh Gupta,Grace Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出利用Neural Radiance Fields（NeRFs）构建用于自主导航的地图，并结合局部与全局信息进行路径规划，提升火星车等自主车辆在未知地形中的导航效率和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前火星车等自主探测器受限于缺乏可高效构建和存储的全局地图，导致在线重规划能力不足，难以实现高效、安全的路径规划。

Method: 利用NeRF从稀疏2D图像中构建高保真3D场景地图，并在此基础上提出一种融合局部代价观测与全局地形特征的路径规划框架；通过在NeRF提取的地形特征上使用核岭回归，实现局部代价在全局区域的插值，支持在线重规划。

Result: 在高保真仿真环境中验证了该方法，相比多种基线方法，实现了更低的路径代价和更高的任务成功率。

Conclusion: NeRF可用于构建适用于自主导航的紧凑型全局地图，所提出的规划框架能有效整合局部感知与全局信息，显著提升行星表面探测任务中路径规划的安全性与效率。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles such as the Mars rovers currently lead the vanguard of surface exploration on extraterrestrial planets and moons. In order to accelerate the pace of exploration and science objectives, it is critical to plan safe and efficient paths for these vehicles. However, current rover autonomy is limited by a lack of global maps which can be easily constructed and stored for onboard re-planning. Recently, Neural Radiance Fields (NeRFs) have been introduced as a detailed 3D scene representation which can be trained from sparse 2D images and efficiently stored. We propose to use NeRFs to construct maps for online use in autonomous navigation, and present a planning framework which leverages the NeRF map to integrate local and global information. Our approach interpolates local cost observations across global regions using kernel ridge regression over terrain features extracted from the NeRF map, allowing the rover to re-route itself around untraversable areas discovered during online operation. We validate our approach in high-fidelity simulation and demonstrate lower cost and higher percentage success rate path planning compared to various baselines.

</details>


### [191] [DexEXO: A Wearability-First Dexterous Exoskeleton for Operator-Agnostic Demonstration and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17323)
*Alvin Zhu,Mingzhang Zhu,Beom Jun Kim,Jose Victor S. H. Ramos,Yike Shi,Yufeng Wu,Raayan Dhar,Fuyi Yang,Ruochen Hou,Hanzhang Fang,Quanyou Wang,Yuchen Cui,Dennis W. Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexEXO是一种以可穿戴性为优先的手部外骨骼设备，通过硬件级的视觉、接触几何和运动学对齐，支持跨用户、高舒适度的操作数据采集，并实现从原始RGB观测直接训练策略，简化了灵巧机器人学习流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可穿戴接口在舒适性和跨用户适应性与运动精度之间难以兼顾，且演示与部署之间的具身差异导致需要额外的视觉后处理，限制了灵巧机器人学习的规模化。

Method: 提出DexEXO，采用面向可穿戴性的设计，包括姿态容错的拇指机构和基于滑块的指部接口，解析建模支持140~217 mm手长范围；设备外观与部署机器人一致，使用腕部RGB相机直接采集视觉对齐的原始数据用于策略训练。

Result: 用户研究表明DexEXO在舒适性和可用性上优于先前系统；仅使用视觉对齐的原始观测即可训练出具有竞争力的扩散策略，显著简化端到端流程。

Conclusion: 优先考虑可穿戴性和硬件级具身对齐可同时减少人类操作和算法处理的瓶颈，且不牺牲任务性能，有助于推动灵巧机器人学习的规模化。

Abstract: Scaling dexterous robot learning is constrained by the difficulty of collecting high-quality demonstrations across diverse operators. Existing wearable interfaces often trade comfort and cross-user adaptability for kinematic fidelity, while embodiment mismatch between demonstration and deployment requires visual post-processing before policy training. We present DexEXO, a wearability-first hand exoskeleton that aligns visual appearance, contact geometry, and kinematics at the hardware level. DexEXO features a pose-tolerant thumb mechanism and a slider-based finger interface analytically modeled to support hand lengths from 140~mm to 217~mm, reducing operator-specific fitting and enabling scalable cross-operator data collection. A passive hand visually matches the deployed robot, allowing direct policy training from raw wrist-mounted RGB observations. User studies demonstrate improved comfort and usability compared to prior wearable systems. Using visually aligned observations alone, we train diffusion policies that achieve competitive performance while substantially simplifying the end-to-end pipeline.
