These results show that prioritizing wearability and hardware-level embodiment alignment reduces both human and algorithmic bottlenecks without sacrificing task performance. Project Page: https://dexexo-research.github.io/

</details>


### [192] [Physics-informed Deep Mixture-of-Koopmans Vehicle Dynamics Model with Dual-branch Encoder for Distributed Electric-drive Trucks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17416)
*Jinyu Miao,Pu Zhang,Rujun Yan,Yifei He,Bowei Zhang,Zheng Fu,Ke Wang,Qi Song,Kun Jiang,Mengmeng Yang,Diange Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Koopman算子理论的全数据驱动车辆动力学建模方法KODE，用于高精度建模分布式电动卡车（DET）的非线性耦合动力学特性。通过双分支编码器和物理信息监督机制提升模型性能，并引入混合Koopman框架以适应多样的驾驶模式，在仿真和实测中均实现了领先的长期状态估计效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于车辆动力学具有强非线性和纵横向耦合特性，传统建模方法在识别效率、精度及与线性控制兼容性方面难以兼顾，尤其对复杂结构的分布式电驱动卡车（DET）更具挑战。

Method: 提出一种基于Koopman算子的全数据驱动建模方法KODE：设计双分支编码器提取动态状态特征；引入基于车辆运动时序几何一致性的物理信息监督机制指导训练；扩展为混合Koopman算子框架以适应多样驾驶模式。

Result: 在TruckSim高保真仿真环境和真实世界实验中验证了所提方法的有效性，结果表明该方法在长期动力学状态估计任务中优于现有方法，达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的KODE方法能够有效处理DET车辆的高度非线性与耦合动力学建模问题，在保证线性结构的同时实现高精度预测，兼具良好的控制兼容潜力，为数据驱动车辆建模提供了新思路。

Abstract: Advanced autonomous driving systems require accurate vehicle dynamics modeling. However, identifying a precise dynamics model remains challenging due to strong nonlinearities and the coupled longitudinal and lateral dynamic characteristics. Previous research has employed physics-based analytical models or neural networks to construct vehicle dynamics representations. Nevertheless, these approaches often struggle to simultaneously achieve satisfactory performance in terms of system identification efficiency, modeling accuracy, and compatibility with linear control strategies. In this paper, we propose a fully data-driven dynamics modeling method tailored for complex distributed electric-drive trucks (DETs), leveraging Koopman operator theory to represent highly nonlinear dynamics in a lifted linear embedding space. To achieve high-precision modeling, we first propose a novel dual-branch encoder which encodes dynamic states and provides a powerful basis for the proposed Koopman-based methods entitled KODE. A physics-informed supervision mechanism, grounded in the geometric consistency of temporal vehicle motion, is incorporated into the training process to facilitate effective learning of both the encoder and the Koopman operator. Furthermore, to accommodate the diverse driving patterns of DETs, we extend the vanilla Koopman operator to a mixture-of-Koopman operator framework, enhancing modeling capability. Simulations conducted in a high-fidelity TruckSim environment and real-world experiments demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves state-of-the-art performance in long-term dynamics state estimation.

</details>


### [193] [SafeLand: Safe Autonomous Landing in Unknown Environments with Bayesian Semantic Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17430)
*Markus Gross,Andreas Greiner,Sai Bharadhwaj Matha,Felix Soest,Daniel Cremers,Henri Meeß*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SafeLand是一种基于视觉的轻量级无人机安全自主降落系统，无需先验信息，仅使用摄像头和轻量高度传感器，通过语义分割与贝叶斯滤波实现实时动态环境下的可靠着陆点识别，并通过行为树机制应对动态障碍物，确保高安全性和实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机降落方法依赖先验地图、重型传感器或静态标记，难以应对未知动态环境中的人类等非合作障碍物，限制了在城市和乡村场景中的广泛应用。因此需要一种轻量化、不依赖先验信息且能保障人类安全的自主降落方案。

Method: 提出SafeLand系统：利用深度学习进行语义分割构建在线语义地面图，结合贝叶斯概率滤波与时间语义衰减提升稳定性；通过行为树控制自适应降落过程，实时检测并响应动态障碍物（如暂停、爬升或重规划）；系统仅需相机和轻量高度传感器，优化部署于嵌入式平台。

Result: 在200次仿真和60次实地测试中验证，覆盖工业、城市和乡村环境，最高达100米高度，实现人类检测零漏报（零假阴性），响应延迟低于一秒，成功率高达95%，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SafeLand实现了高效、安全、轻量化的无人机自主降落，能够在无先验信息的动态复杂环境中可靠运行，显著提升对人类和基础设施的安全保障，具备良好的实际部署潜力，并已开源其分割模型以促进空中机器人研究发展。

Abstract: Autonomous landing of uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs) in unknown, dynamic environments poses significant safety challenges, particularly near people and infrastructure, as UAVs transition to routine urban and rural operations. Existing methods often rely on prior maps, heavy sensors like LiDAR, static markers, or fail to handle non-cooperative dynamic obstacles like humans, limiting generalization and real-time performance. To address these challenges, we introduce SafeLand, a lean, vision-based system for safe autonomous landing (SAL) that requires no prior information and operates only with a camera and a lightweight height sensor. Our approach constructs an online semantic ground map via deep learning-based semantic segmentation, optimized for embedded deployment and trained on a consolidation of seven curated public aerial datasets (achieving 70.22% mIoU across 20 classes), which is further refined through Bayesian probabilistic filtering with temporal semantic decay to robustly identify metric-scale landing spots. A behavior tree then governs adaptive landing, iteratively validates the spot, and reacts in real time to dynamic obstacles by pausing, climbing, or rerouting to alternative spots, maximizing human safety. We extensively evaluate our method in 200 simulations and 60 end-to-end field tests across industrial, urban, and rural environments at altitudes up to 100m, demonstrating zero false negatives for human detection. Compared to the state of the art, SafeLand achieves sub-second response latency, substantially lower than previous methods, while maintaining a superior success rate of 95%. To facilitate further research in aerial robotics, we release SafeLand's segmentation model as a plug-and-play ROS package, available at https://github.com/markus-42/SafeLand.

</details>


### [194] [P$^{3}$Nav: End-to-End Perception, Prediction and Planning for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17459)
*Tianfu Li,Wenbo Chen,Haoxuan Xu,Xinhu Zheng,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出P$^{3}$Nav框架，通过整合感知、预测和规划，提升视觉-语言导航中的场景理解与导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言导航方法忽视了在规划前进行充分场景理解的重要性，导致感知与预测能力不足。

Method: 设计端到端的P$^{3}$Nav框架，融合对象级与地图级感知、未来路径点预测及语义地图线索预测，实现更优的导航规划。

Result: 在REVERIE、R2R-CE和RxR-CE基准上取得当前最优性能。

Conclusion: P$^{3}$Nav通过联合感知、预测与规划，显著增强了智能体的场景理解与导航成功率。

Abstract: In Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN), an agent is required to plan a path to the target specified by the language instruction, using its visual observations. Consequently, prevailing VLN methods primarily focus on building powerful planners through visual-textual alignment. However, these approaches often bypass the imperative of comprehensive scene understanding prior to planning, leaving the agent with insufficient perception or prediction capabilities. Thus, we propose P$^{3}$Nav, a novel end-to-end framework integrating perception, prediction, and planning in a unified pipeline to strengthen the VLN agent's scene understanding and boost navigation success. Specifically, P$^{3}$Nav augments perception by extracting complementary cues from object-level and map-level perspectives. Subsequently, our P$^{3}$Nav predicts waypoints to model the agent's potential future states, endowing the agent with intrinsic awareness of candidate positions during navigation. Conditioned on these future waypoints, P$^{3}$Nav further forecasts semantic map cues, enabling proactive planning and reducing the strict reliance on purely historical context. Integrating these perceptual and predictive cues, a holistic planning module finally carries out the VLN tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our P$^{3}$Nav achieves new state-of-the-art performance on the REVERIE, R2R-CE, and RxR-CE benchmarks.

</details>


### [195] [From Optimizable to Interactable: Mixed Digital Twin-Empowered Testing of Vehicle-Infrastructure Cooperation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17497)
*Jianghong Dong,Chunying Yang,Mengchi Cai,Chaoyi Chen,Qing Xu,Jianqiang Wang,Keqiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种L5级可交互的车路协同系统数字孪生测试框架IMPACT，通过引入人机交互和虚实联动机制，在真实环境中生成高质量的极端案例，提升系统测试的有效性与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车路协同系统（VICS）的极端案例生成主要依赖AI驱动方法，且测试多局限于仿真环境，难以覆盖真实世界中高度不确定的人类行为，因此需要一种能融合真实交互与物理环境的新型测试框架。

Method: 提出了L5级“可交互”数字孪生架构，并设计了IMPACT测试框架，支持人类直接与VICS实体交互，结合混合数字孪生技术实现“物理-虚拟动作交互”，在包含真实环境的条件下进行安全测试。

Result: 在I-VIT测试平台上实现了IMPACT框架，实验验证了其在生成高质量极端案例方面的有效性，并提供了实验视频作为佐证。

Conclusion: IMPACT通过引入人类交互和虚实融合机制，突破了传统仿真测试的局限，为车路协同系统的长期可靠运行提供了更全面、安全的测试手段。

Abstract: Sufficient testing under corner cases is critical for the long-term operation of vehicle-infrastructure cooperation systems (VICS). However, existing corner-case generation methods are primarily AI-driven, and VICS testing under corner cases is typically limited to simulation. In this paper, we introduce an L5 ''Interactable'' level to the VICS digital twin (VICS-DT) taxonomy, extending beyond the conventional L4 ''Optimizable'' level. We further propose an L5-level VICS testing framework, IMPACT (Interactive Mixed-digital-twin Paradigm for Advanced Cooperative vehicle-infrastructure Testing). By enabling direct human interactions with VICS entities, IMPACT incorporates highly uncertain and unpredictable human behaviors into the testing loop, naturally generating high-quality corner cases that complement AI-based methods. Furthermore, the mixedDT-enabled ''Physical-Virtual Action Interaction'' facilitates safe VICS testing under corner cases, incorporating real-world environments and entities rather than purely in simulation. Finally, we implement IMPACT on the I-VIT (Interactive Vehicle-Infrastructure Testbed), and experiments demonstrate its effectiveness. The experimental videos are available at our project website: https://dongjh20.github.io/IMPACT.

</details>


### [196] [Interpreting Context-Aware Human Preferences for Multi-Objective Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17510)
*Tharun Sethuraman,Subham Agrawal,Nils Dengler,Jorge de Heuvel,Teena Hassan,Maren Bennewitz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合基础模型与多目标强化学习（MORL）的机器人导航框架，使机器人能够理解并应用基于上下文的自然语言导航偏好，实现在人类共享环境中的自适应、透明且安全的实时行为控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在人类共享环境中需不仅满足安全性与效率等任务目标，还需根据自然语言表达的、依赖环境上下文的人类偏好调整行为；然而这类偏好难以直接融入低层控制策略，因此需要一种能桥接高层语义理解与底层运动控制的解决方案。

Method: 该方法采用视觉-语言模型（VLM）从视觉观测中提取环境上下文，利用大语言模型（LLM）将自然语言反馈转化为可解释的、上下文相关的规则，并存储于可更新的规则记忆中；通过偏好翻译模块将上下文与规则映射为数值型偏好向量，进而参数化预训练的MORL导航策略以实现实时行为适应。

Result: 通过组件级定量评估、用户研究和真实机器人部署验证了框架有效性，结果表明系统能可靠捕捉用户意图、生成一致的偏好向量，并在多种室内环境中实现可控的行为适应。

Conclusion: 所提出的管道有效整合了高层语义推理与低层运动控制，提升了机器人在人类共享环境中的适应性、透明性和可用性，同时保持了安全且响应迅速的实时控制能力。

Abstract: Robots operating in human-shared environments must not only achieve task-level navigation objectives such as safety and efficiency, but also adapt their behavior to human preferences. However, as human preferences are typically expressed in natural language and depend on environmental context, it is difficult to directly integrate them into low-level robot control policies. In this work, we present a pipeline that enables robots to understand and apply context-dependent navigation preferences by combining foundational models with a Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning (MORL) navigation policy. Thus, our approach integrates high-level semantic reasoning with low-level motion control. A Vision-Language Model (VLM) extracts structured environmental context from onboard visual observations, while Large Language Models (LLM) convert natural language user feedback into interpretable, context-dependent behavioral rules stored in a persistent but updatable rule memory. A preference translation module then maps contextual information and stored rules into numerical preference vectors that parameterize a pretrained MORL policy for real-time navigation adaptation. We evaluate the proposed framework through quantitative component-level evaluations, a user study, and real-world robot deployments in various indoor environments. Our results demonstrate that the system reliably captures user intent, generates consistent preference vectors, and enables controllable behavior adaptation across diverse contexts. Overall, the proposed pipeline improves the adaptability, transparency, and usability of robots operating in shared human environments, while maintaining safe and responsive real-time control.

</details>


### [197] [KineVLA: Towards Kinematics-Aware Vision-Language-Action Models with Bi-Level Action Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17524)
*Gaoge Han,Zhengqing Gao,Ziwen Li,Jiaxin Huang,Shaoli Huang,Fakhri Karray,Mingming Gong,Tongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的包含丰富运动学信息的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）任务，并设计了KineVLA框架以解耦目标不变性与运动学可变性，通过双层次表示和推理令牌实现更精细、可控和泛化的操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作指令对运动学特征的描述粗糙或不完整，难以支持细粒度和个性化的操作需求，因此需要一种能精确编码运动学属性的语言指令机制。

Method: 提出KineVLA框架，采用双层次动作表示和双层次推理令牌，显式分离目标级不变性与运动学级可变性，并构建包含仿真与真实机器人平台的运动学感知VLA数据集进行训练与验证。

Result: 在LIBERO和Realman-75机器人平台上实验表明，KineVLA在运动学敏感基准上持续优于强基线模型，实现了更精确、可控和可泛化的操作行为。

Conclusion: KineVLA通过显式的双层次建模有效桥接了语言指令中的细粒度运动学描述与实际动作执行，为个性化和高精度的机器人操作提供了新思路。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a novel kinematics-rich vision-language-action (VLA) task, in which language commands densely encode diverse kinematic attributes (such as direction, trajectory, orientation, and relative displacement) from initiation through completion, at key moments, unlike existing action instructions that capture kinematics only coarsely or partially, thereby supporting fine-grained and personalized manipulation. In this setting, where task goals remain invariant while execution trajectories must adapt to instruction-level kinematic specifications. To address this challenge, we propose KineVLA, a vision-language-action framework that explicitly decouples goal-level invariance from kinematics-level variability through a bi-level action representation and bi-level reasoning tokens to serve as explicit, supervised intermediate variables that align language and action. To support this task, we construct the kinematics-aware VLA datasets spanning both simulation and real-world robotic platforms, featuring instruction-level kinematic variations and bi-level annotations. Extensive experiments on LIBERO and a Realman-75 robot demonstrate that KineVLA consistently outperforms strong VLA baselines on kinematics-sensitive benchmarks, achieving more precise, controllable, and generalizable manipulation behaviors.

</details>


### [198] [HeiSD: Hybrid Speculative Decoding for Embodied Vision-Language-Action Models with Kinematic Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17573)
*Zihao Zheng,Zhihao Mao,Sicheng Tian,Maoliang Li,Jiayu Chen,Xinhao Sun,Zhaobo Zhang,Xuanzhe Liu,Donggang Cao,Hong Mei,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于加速视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型推理的混合推测解码框架HeiSD，通过结合drafter-based和retrieval-based方法，并引入验证跳过机制、序列级宽松接受策略以及基于运动学的融合度量，实现了显著的速度提升同时保持高任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推测解码方法未能分析drafter-based和retrieval-based在VLA模型中的优劣，导致只能单独应用或优化其中一种，限制了推理加速效果。

Method: 提出HeiSD框架：1）设计验证跳过机制和序列级宽松接受策略以优化retrieval-based SD；2）提出基于运动学的融合度量来自动生成混合边界，实现两类SD方法的动态结合。

Result: 实验结果显示，HeiSD在模拟基准中最高可达2.45倍加速，在真实场景中达到2.06~2.41倍加速，同时保持高任务成功率。

Conclusion: 应以混合方式使用两类推测解码方法，HeiSD有效解决了其在VLA模型中应用的关键挑战，显著提升了推理速度与稳定性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) Models have become the mainstream solution for robot control, but suffer from slow inference speeds. Speculative Decoding (SD) is a promising acceleration method which can be divided into two categories: drafter-based SD and retrieval-based SD. Existing methods fail to analyze the advantages and disadvantages of these two types of SD in VLA models, leading to their sole application or optimization. In this paper, we analyze the trajectory patterns of robots controlled by the VLA model and derive a key insight: the two types of SD should be used in a hybrid manner. However, achieving hybrid SD in VLA models poses several challenges: (1) draft rejection and persistent errors in retrieval-based SD; (2) difficulty in determining the hybrid boundary. To address these, we propose the HeiSD framework. We propose a retrieval-based SD optimization method in HeiSD,which contains a verify-skip mechanism and a sequence-wise relaxed acceptance strategy. Moreover, we proposed a kinematic-based fused metric in HeiSD to automatically determine the hybrid boundary. Experimental results demonstrate that HeiSD attains a speedup of up to 2.45x in simulation benchmarks and 2.06x~2.41x in real-world scenarios, while sustaining a high task success rate.

</details>


### [199] [VectorWorld: Efficient Streaming World Model via Diffusion Flow on Vector Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17652)
*Chaokang Jiang,Desen Zhou,Jiuming Liu,Kevin Li Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为VectorWorld的流式世界模型，用于自动驾驶策略的闭环评估，通过历史条件化初始化、实时单步补全和物理对齐的非自车策略ΔSim，实现了高保真、长距离、实时的闭环仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式世界模型在闭环评估中存在初始化不匹配、多步采样延迟和长时间轨迹下运动不可行性累积等问题，难以满足自动驾驶策略评估的需求。

Method: 提出VectorWorld，采用运动感知门控VAE进行历史条件化初始化，利用边缘门控关系DiT实现无求解器的单步掩码补全，并引入ΔSim这一物理对齐的混合动作空间NPC策略，结合区间条件MeanFlow与基于JVP的大步长监督训练。

Result: 在Waymo开放运动数据集和nuPlan上验证，VectorWorld提升了地图结构保真度和初始化有效性，支持超过1公里的稳定、实时闭环仿真。

Conclusion: VectorWorld通过流式生成局部向量图块、实现实时交互补全和物理合理的NPC行为建模，有效解决了现有世界模型在闭环评估中的三大瓶颈，为自动驾驶仿真提供了高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Closed-loop evaluation of autonomous-driving policies requires interactive simulation beyond log replay. However, existing generative world models often degrade in closed loop due to (i) history-free initialization that mismatches policy inputs, (ii) multi-step sampling latency that violates real-time budgets, and (iii) compounding kinematic infeasibility over long horizons. We propose VectorWorld, a streaming world model that incrementally generates ego-centric $64 \mathrm{m}\times 64\mathrm{m}$ lane--agent vector-graph tiles during rollout. VectorWorld aligns initialization with history-conditioned policies by producing a policy-compatible interaction state via a motion-aware gated VAE. It enables real-time outpainting via solver-free one-step masked completion with an edge-gated relational DiT trained with interval-conditioned MeanFlow and JVP-based large-step supervision. To stabilize long-horizon rollouts, we introduce $Δ$Sim, a physics-aligned non-ego (NPC) policy with hybrid discrete--continuous actions and differentiable kinematic logit shaping. On Waymo open motion and nuPlan, VectorWorld improves map-structure fidelity and initialization validity, and supports stable, real-time $1\mathrm{km}+$ closed-loop rollouts (\href{https://github.com/jiangchaokang/VectorWorld}{code}).

</details>


### [200] [Consistency-Driven Dual LSTM Models for Kinematic Control of a Wearable Soft Robotic Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17672)
*Xingyu Chen,Yi Xiong,Li Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于一致性驱动的双LSTM框架，用于准确学习可穿戴软体机器人臂的正向和逆向运动学，有效建模其非线性与迟滞特性，并通过循环一致性损失提升预测稳定性与物理真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体气动执行器具有强非线性和迟滞特性，且存在输入到末端位置的一对多映射问题，传统运动学建模方法难以准确描述其动力学行为，尤其在可穿戴人机交互场景中对精度和物理一致性要求更高。

Method: 设计了一种一致性驱动的双LSTM框架，分别学习正向和逆向运动学，并引入循环一致性损失以约束模型输出，增强物理真实性和预测稳定性。通过轨迹跟踪、消融实验和可穿戴任务验证方法有效性。

Result: 实验表明，所提方法显著提高了预测精度，在轨迹跟踪任务中表现优于传统方法；循环一致性损失有效提升了模型的稳定性和物理一致性；在物体传递、避障抓取和抽屉操作等可穿戴任务中展示了良好的人机协作能力。

Conclusion: 基于学习的一致性驱动运动学建模方法为面向人体的可穿戴软体机器人系统提供了高效且鲁棒的解决方案，展现了其在日常人机交互任务中的广泛应用潜力。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a consistency-driven dual LSTM framework for accurately learning both the forward and inverse kinematics of a pneumatically actuated soft robotic arm integrated into a wearable device. This approach effectively captures the nonlinear and hysteretic behaviors of soft pneumatic actuators while addressing the one-to-many mapping challenge between actuation inputs and end-effector positions. By incorporating a cycle consistency loss, we enhance physical realism and improve the stability of inverse predictions. Extensive experiments-including trajectory tracking, ablation studies, and wearable demonstrations-confirm the effectiveness of our method. Results indicate that the inclusion of the consistency loss significantly boosts prediction accuracy and promotes physical consistency over conventional approaches. Moreover, the wearable soft robotic arm demonstrates strong human-robot collaboration capabilities and adaptability in everyday tasks such as object handover, obstacle-aware pick-and-place, and drawer operation. This work underscores the promising potential of learning-based kinematic models for human-centric, wearable robotic systems.

</details>


### [201] [AERR-Nav: Adaptive Exploration-Recovery-Reminiscing Strategy for Zero-Shot Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17712)
*Jingzhi Huang,Junkai Huang,Haoyang Yang,Haoang Li,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为AERR-Nav的零样本对象导航框架，通过自适应探索-恢复-回想策略和快慢思维模式，在未知多楼层环境中实现了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理未知多楼层环境中的零样本对象导航时难以平衡探索与利用，常导致机器人陷入困境或无法找到楼梯入口。

Method: 设计了自适应探索-恢复-回想策略，并引入具有快慢思维模式的自适应探索状态，以动态调整机器人在不同环境下的行为。

Result: 在HM3D和MP3D基准上的实验表明，AERR-Nav在零样本方法中达到了最先进水平，消融研究验证了各模块的有效性。

Conclusion: AERR-Nav能有效提升机器人在复杂未知环境中的导航能力，特别是在多楼层场景下表现出更强的鲁棒性和效率。

Abstract: Zero-Shot Object Navigation (ZSON) in unknown multi-floor environments presents a significant challenge. Recent methods, mostly based on semantic value greedy waypoint selection, spatial topology-enhanced memory, and Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) as a decision-making framework, have led to improvements. However, these architectures struggle to balance exploration and exploitation for ZSON when encountering unseen environments, especially in multi-floor settings, such as robots getting stuck at narrow intersections, endlessly wandering, or failing to find stair entrances. To overcome these challenges, we propose AERR-Nav, a Zero-Shot Object Navigation framework that dynamically adjusts its state based on the robot's environment. Specifically, AERR-Nav has the following two key advantages: (1) An Adaptive Exploration-Recovery-Reminiscing Strategy, enables robots to dynamically transition between three states, facilitating specialized responses to diverse navigation scenarios. (2) An Adaptive Exploration State featuring Fast and Slow-Thinking modes helps robots better balance exploration, exploitation, and higher-level reasoning based on evolving environmental information. Extensive experiments on the HM3D and MP3D benchmarks demonstrate that our AERR-Nav achieves state-of-the-art performance among zero-shot methods. Comprehensive ablation studies further validate the efficacy of our proposed strategy and modules.

</details>


### [202] [VolumeDP: Modeling Volumetric Representation for Manipulation Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17720)
*Tianxing Zhou,Feiyang Xue,Zhangchen Ye,Tianyuan Yuan,Hang Zhao,Tao Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VolumeDP是一种新的策略架构，通过显式地在3D空间中进行推理来解决视觉模仿学习中的2D-3D不匹配问题，提升了机器人操作任务的空间对齐性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉模仿方法将2D图像观测直接映射到3D动作输出，存在2D-3D空间不匹配的问题，限制了空间推理能力并降低了模型鲁棒性。

Method: 提出VolumeDP，首先通过交叉注意力机制将图像特征提升为体素化表示（Volumetric Representation），然后用可学习模块选择任务相关的体素并转换为紧凑的空间标记集，最后通过多标记解码器基于整个标记集预测动作，避免信息丢失。

Result: 在LIBERO模拟基准上达到88.8%的平均成功率，比最强基线提升14.8%；在ManiSkill和LIBERO-Plus基准上也显著优于先前方法；真实世界实验显示其在新空间布局、相机视角和环境背景下的高成功率和强泛化能力。

Conclusion: VolumeDP通过显式的3D空间推理有效解决了2D-3D不匹配问题，显著提升了模仿学习在仿真和真实场景中的性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Imitation learning is a prominent paradigm for robotic manipulation. However, existing visual imitation methods map 2D image observations directly to 3D action outputs, imposing a 2D-3D mismatch that hinders spatial reasoning and degrades robustness. We present VolumeDP, a policy architecture that restores spatial alignment by explicitly reasoning in 3D. VolumeDP first lifts image features into a Volumetric Representation via cross-attention. It then selects task-relevant voxels with a learnable module and converts them into a compact set of spatial tokens, markedly reducing computation while preserving action-critical geometry. Finally, a multi-token decoder conditions on the entire token set to predict actions, thereby avoiding lossy aggregation that collapses multiple spatial tokens into a single descriptor. VolumeDP achieves a state-of-the-art average success rate of 88.8% on the LIBERO simulation benchmark, outperforming the strongest baseline by a substantial 14.8% improvement. It also delivers large performance gains over prior methods on the ManiSkill and LIBERO-Plus benchmarks. Real-world experiments further demonstrate higher success rates and robust generalization to novel spatial layouts, camera viewpoints, and environment backgrounds. Code will be released.

</details>


### [203] [Multi-Source Human-in-the-Loop Digital Twin Testbed for Connected and Autonomous Vehicles in Mixed Traffic Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17751)
*Jianghong Dong,Jiawei Wang,Chunying Yang,Mengchi Cai,Chaoyi Chen,Qing Xu,Jianqiang Wang,Keqiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MSH-MCCT的新型CAV测试平台，结合混合数字孪生技术，实现物理、虚拟和混合环境中自动驾驶车辆与人类驾驶车辆的实时交互，提升了实验灵活性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在混合交通环境中，自动驾驶车辆需要与人类驾驶车辆进行复杂交互，现有测试平台难以全面模拟此类多源交互场景。

Method: 提出MSH-MCCT测试平台，融合混合现实与数字孪生技术，集成物理、虚拟和混合平台，并引入多源控制输入，支持多视角下人驾与自动驾驶系统的协同操作。

Result: 实验验证了该平台在混合交通车辆编队中的有效性，实现了物理与虚拟CAV及HDV的共存与实时交互，支持不同保真度驾驶模拟器中多源人类驾驶员参与测试。

Conclusion: MSH-MCCT显著提升了自动驾驶车辆在复杂混合交通环境下的测试能力，具有良好的灵活性、可扩展性和应用前景。

Abstract: In the emerging mixed traffic environments, Connected and Autonomous Vehicles (CAVs) have to interact with surrounding human-driven vehicles (HDVs). This paper introduces MSH-MCCT (Multi-Source Human-in-the-Loop Mixed Cloud Control Testbed), a novel CAV testbed that captures complex interactions between various CAVs and HDVs. Utilizing the Mixed Digital Twin concept, which combines Mixed Reality with Digital Twin, MSH-MCCT integrates physical, virtual, and mixed platforms, along with multi-source control inputs. Bridged by the mixed platform, MSH-MCCT allows human drivers and CAV algorithms to operate both physical and virtual vehicles within multiple fields of view. Particularly, this testbed facilitates the coexistence and real-time interaction of physical and virtual CAVs \& HDVs, significantly enhancing the experimental flexibility and scalability. Experiments on vehicle platooning in mixed traffic showcase the potential of MSH-MCCT to conduct CAV testing with multi-source real human drivers in the loop through driving simulators of diverse fidelity. The videos for the experiments are available at our project website: https://dongjh20.github.io/MSH-MCCT.

</details>


### [204] [Huddle: Parallel Shape Assembly using Decentralized, Minimalistic Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17768)
*Khai Yi Chin,Tingwei Meng,Zhe Chen,Daniel Bassett,Yuri Ivanov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于局部交互的去中心化机器人算法，可实现任意形状的自主组装，无需全局控制或精确定位，并在107个机器人的实验中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决去中心化机器人在形成任意形状组装时可能出现的不可达状态和装配间隙问题，需要一种仅依赖局部通信且具备全局一致性的算法。

Method: 通过在已组装机器人之间传递信号并利用简单的对齐机制吸引路过机器人加入，仅依赖相邻节点间的通信，不依赖运动模式或姿态定位，实现异步、顺序无关的组装过程。

Result: 算法被证明是正确的，并成功实现了由107个机器人组成的复杂形状组装，过程中无间隙或死锁状态。

Conclusion: 该方法是一种极简、鲁棒且可扩展的去中心化组装策略，适用于大规模、异构或动态环境下的机器人集群应用。

Abstract: We propose a novel algorithm for forming arbitrarily shaped assemblies using decentralized robots. By relying on local interactions, the algorithm ensures there are no unreachable states or gaps in the assembly, which are global properties. The in-assembly robots attract passing-by robots into expanding the assembly via a simple implementation of signaling and alignment. Our approach is minimalistic, requiring only communication between attached, immediate neighbors. It is motion-agnostic and requires no pose localization, enabling asynchronous and order-independent assembly. We prove the algorithm's correctness and demonstrate its effectiveness in forming a 107-robot assembly.

</details>


### [205] [EVA: Aligning Video World Models with Executable Robot Actions via Inverse Dynamics Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17808)
*Ruixiang Wang,Qingming Liu,Yueci Deng,Guiliang Liu,Zhen Liu,Kui Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为可执行视频对齐（EVA）的强化学习后训练框架，用于缩小视觉生成与物理可执行控制之间的“可执行性差距”，通过利用逆向动力学模型作为奖励模型来评估生成视频的动作序列，从而提高机器人任务执行的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频世界模型缺乏明确的可执行性约束，导致生成的视觉序列可能违反刚体和运动学一致性，从而在解码为控制命令时产生不稳定或不可行的动作。这种视觉生成与物理可执行控制之间的不匹配被称为“可执行性差距”。

Method: 本文提出了Executable Video Alignment（EVA）框架，采用强化学习进行后训练，使用在真实机器人轨迹上训练的逆向动力学模型（IDM）作为奖励模型，评估生成视频所诱导的动作序列，并鼓励平滑运动（如速度、加速度和加加速度），同时惩罚违反具身约束的动作。该奖励信号即使在视频存在严重视觉伪影时仍保持有效。

Result: 在RoboTwin基准和真实双臂机器人上的实验表明，EVA减少了生成rollout中的具身相关伪影，并提升了下游任务的执行成功率。

Conclusion: EVA通过将可执行性差距转化为训练信号，有效提升了视频世界模型在机器人控制中的实用性与稳定性，增强了生成动作的物理合理性与任务完成能力。

Abstract: Video generative models are increasingly used as world models for robotics, where a model generates a future visual rollout conditioned on the current observation and task instruction, and an inverse dynamics model (IDM) converts the generated frames into executable robot actions. However, current video world models lack explicit executability constraints. As a result, visually coherent rollouts may still violate rigid-body and kinematic consistency, producing unstable or infeasible control commands when decoded by an IDM. We refer to this mismatch between visual generation and physically executable control as the executability gap. While this gap can be mitigated at inference time using techniques such as rejection sampling, such approaches are inefficient due to the high cost of video generation. In this paper, we leverage the executability gap as a training signal and introduce Executable Video Alignment (EVA), a reinforcement-learning post-training framework for aligning video world models. EVA trains an inverse dynamics model on real robot trajectories and repurposes it as a reward model that evaluates generated videos through the action sequences they induce, encouraging smooth motions measured by velocity, acceleration, and jerk while penalizing actions that violate embodiment constraints. Importantly, the reward remains informative even when generated videos contain severe visual artifacts, since such artifacts typically translate into unstable or out-of-bound actions. Experiments on the RoboTwin benchmark and a real bimanual robot show that EVA reduces embodiment-specific artifacts in generated rollouts and improves downstream task execution success.

</details>


### [206] [Generative Control as Optimization: Time Unconditional Flow Matching for Adaptive and Robust Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17834)
*Zunzhe Zhang,Runhan Huang,Yicheng Liu,Shaoting Zhu,Linzhan Mou,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Generative Control as Optimization (GeCO)，一种时间无条件的框架，将扩散模型和流匹配中的动作生成从轨迹积分转变为迭代优化，实现计算资源的自适应分配，并提供无需训练的内在安全信号用于异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型和流匹配方法在推理时使用固定的积分调度，无法根据状态复杂性调整计算量，导致对简单和复杂任务投入相同的计算资源，效率低下且缺乏安全机制。

Method: GeCO通过学习动作序列空间中的一个静态速度场，使专家行为成为稳定吸引子；测试时采用基于收敛性的自适应迭代优化，动态决定退出时机，并利用速度场范数作为无需训练的OOD检测信号。

Result: 在标准仿真基准和pi0系列视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型上验证了GeCO的有效性，作为即插即用模块提升了成功率和推理效率，并实现了安全部署。

Conclusion: GeCO为机器人模仿学习提供了一种更高效、自适应且安全的动作生成范式，打破了传统方法对固定推理步骤的依赖。

Abstract: Diffusion models and flow matching have become a cornerstone of robotic imitation learning, yet they suffer from a structural inefficiency where inference is often bound to a fixed integration schedule that is agnostic to state complexity. This paradigm forces the policy to expend the same computational budget on trivial motions as it does on complex tasks. We introduce Generative Control as Optimization (GeCO), a time-unconditional framework that transforms action synthesis from trajectory integration into iterative optimization. GeCO learns a stationary velocity field in the action-sequence space where expert behaviors form stable attractors. Consequently, test-time inference becomes an adaptive process that allocates computation based on convergence--exiting early for simple states while refining longer for difficult ones. Furthermore, this stationary geometry yields an intrinsic, training-free safety signal, as the field norm at the optimized action serves as a robust out-of-distribution (OOD) detector, remaining low for in-distribution states while significantly increasing for anomalies. We validate GeCO on standard simulation benchmarks and demonstrate seamless scaling to pi0-series Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. As a plug-and-play replacement for standard flow-matching heads, GeCO improves success rates and efficiency with an optimization-native mechanism for safe deployment. Video and code can be found at https://hrh6666.github.io/GeCO/

</details>


### [207] [DexViTac: Collecting Human Visuo-Tactile-Kinematic Demonstrations for Contact-Rich Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17851)
*Xitong Chen,Yifeng Pan,Min Li,Xiaotian Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DexViTac的便携式、以人为中心的数据采集系统，用于高保真地收集接触丰富的灵巧操作中的视觉、触觉和运动学数据，并结合一种基于运动学的触觉表示学习算法，显著提升了机器人在复杂任务中的操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的以人为中心的数据采集系统难以捕捉物理交互过程中的触觉信息，限制了机器人学习接触丰富的灵巧操作的能力。

Method: 设计了一个集成第一人称视觉、高密度触觉传感、末端执行器位姿和手部运动学的硬件系统DexViTac，并提出一种基于运动学的触觉表示学习算法以消除触觉信号中的语义歧义。

Result: 构建了包含2400多个多模态演示的数据集，系统采集效率超过每小时248次演示，在真实场景中对四个挑战性任务的平均成功率超过85%，显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: DexViTac系统及其学习策略有效提升了接触丰富灵巧操作的学习性能，验证了高质量多模态数据在机器人学习中的关键作用。

Abstract: Large-scale, high-quality multimodal demonstrations are essential for robot learning of contact-rich dexterous manipulation. While human-centric data collection systems lower the barrier to scaling, they struggle to capture the tactile information during physical interactions. Motivated by this, we present DexViTac, a portable, human-centric data collection system tailored for contact-rich dexterous manipulation. The system enables the high-fidelity acquisition of first-person vision, high-density tactile sensing, end-effector poses, and hand kinematics within unstructured, in-the-wild environments. Building upon this hardware, we propose a kinematics-grounded tactile representation learning algorithm that effectively resolves semantic ambiguities within tactile signals. Leveraging the efficiency of DexViTac, we construct a multimodal dataset comprising over 2,400 visuo-tactile-kinematic demonstrations. Experiments demonstrate that DexViTac achieves a collection efficiency exceeding 248 demonstrations per hour and remains robust against complex visual occlusions. Real-world deployment confirms that policies trained with the proposed dataset and learning strategy achieve an average success rate exceeding 85% across four challenging tasks. This performance significantly outperforms baseline methods, thereby validating the substantial improvement the system provides for learning contact-rich dexterous manipulation. Project page: https://xitong-c.github.io/DexViTac/.

</details>


### [208] [RoboForge: Physically Optimized Text-guided Whole-Body Locomotion for Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17927)
*Xichen Yuan,Zhe Li,Bofan Lyu,Kuangji Zuo,Yanshuo Lu,Gen Li,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的隐空间驱动框架，通过双向物理优化实现文本到人形机器人运动的生成与控制耦合，无需重定向即可生成物理上合理且可执行的动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本生成人体运动的方法在迁移到真实人形机器人时面临物理不可行性、接触错误和动力学数据稀缺等问题，难以保证动作的可执行性和稳定性。

Method: 设计了一个物理合理性优化（PP-Opt）模块，作为生成与控制之间的双向耦合接口：前向过程中通过教师-学生策略蒸馏结合合理性奖励抑制漂浮、滑动等伪影；反向过程中将优化后的仿真轨迹转化为高质量显式动作数据，用于微调运动生成器。

Result: 在Unitree G1机器人上实验表明，该方法显著提升了动作跟踪精度和任务成功率；在IsaacLab和MuJoCo环境中，隐式生成管道在精确性和稳定性上均优于传统显式重定向基线。

Conclusion: 通过将扩散模型生成与物理合理性优化相结合，该框架为可部署的文本引导人形智能提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: While generative models have become effective at producing human-like motions from text, transferring these motions to humanoid robots for physical execution remains challenging. Existing pipelines are often limited by retargeting, where kinematic quality is undermined by physical infeasibility, contact-transition errors, and the high cost of real-world dynamical data. We present a unified latent-driven framework that bridges natural language and whole-body humanoid locomotion through a retarget-free, physics-optimized pipeline. Rather than treating generation and control as separate stages, our key insight is to couple them bidirectionally under physical constraints.We introduce a Physical Plausibility Optimization (PP-Opt) module as the coupling interface. In the forward direction, PP-Opt refines a teacher-student distillation policy with a plausibility-centric reward to suppress artifacts such as floating, skating, and penetration. In the backward direction, it converts reward-optimized simulation rollouts into high-quality explicit motion data, which is used to fine-tune the motion generator toward a more physically plausible latent distribution. This bidirectional design forms a self-improving cycle: the generator learns a physically grounded latent space, while the controller learns to execute latent-conditioned behaviors with dynamical integrity.Extensive experiments on the Unitree G1 humanoid show that our bidirectional optimization improves tracking accuracy and success rates. Across IsaacLab and MuJoCo, the implicit latent-driven pipeline consistently outperforms conventional explicit retargeting baselines in both precision and stability. By coupling diffusion-based motion generation with physical plausibility optimization, our framework provides a practical path toward deployable text-guided humanoid intelligence.

</details>


### [209] [Specification-Aware Distribution Shaping for Robotics Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17969)
*Sadık Bera Yüksel,Derya Aksaray*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种在不修改预训练机器人基础模型参数的情况下，通过执行信号时序逻辑（STL）约束来保证自然语言指令执行过程中满足复杂时空规范的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基础模型缺乏对部署过程中安全性以及时变规范的形式化保障，难以满足实际中复杂的时空操作约束。

Method: 提出一种面向规范的行动分布优化框架，在每个决策步骤通过前向动力学传播推理剩余时间范围，计算满足硬性STL可行性约束的最小修改行动分布。

Result: 在多个环境和复杂规范下基于最先进的机器人基础模型进行了仿真验证，结果表明该方法能有效满足STL约束。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在保留预训练模型能力的同时，为机器人执行提供形式化的安全性和时序规范保障。

Abstract: Robotics foundation models have demonstrated strong capabilities in executing natural language instructions across diverse tasks and environments. However, they remain largely data-driven and lack formal guarantees on safety and satisfaction of time-dependent specifications during deployment. In practice, robots often need to comply with operational constraints involving rich spatio-temporal requirements such as time-bounded goal visits, sequential objectives, and persistent safety conditions. In this work, we propose a specification-aware action distribution optimization framework that enforces a broad class of Signal Temporal Logic (STL) constraints during execution of a pretrained robotics foundation model without modifying its parameters. At each decision step, the method computes a minimally modified action distribution that satisfies a hard STL feasibility constraint by reasoning over the remaining horizon using forward dynamics propagation. We validate the proposed framework in simulation using a state-of-the-art robotics foundation model across multiple environments and complex specifications.

</details>


### [210] [A Single-Fiber Optical Frequency Domain Reflectometry (OFDR)-Based Shape Sensing of Concentric Tube Steerable Drilling Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17990)
*Yash Kulkarni,Mobina Tavangarifard,Daniyal Maroufi,Mohsen Khadem,Justin E. Bird,Jeffrey H. Siewerdsen,Farshid Alambeigi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于OFDR的连续光纤形状感知方法，用于同心管可转向钻探机器人，具有高空间分辨率和良好的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于FBG的形状感知方法空间分辨率有限，且需要将传感器粘附于器械表面，限制了其在微创手术中的应用。

Method: 设计并制造了一个形状感知组件（SSA），将单根OFDR光纤与扁平NiTi丝集成，并将其置入CT-SDR内部通道中作为保护鞘，实现无需表面集成的连续应变测量。

Result: 在自由弯曲条件和多种J形轨迹钻探过程中对SSA进行了测试，结果表明该方法能够准确、可靠地重建器械形状。

Conclusion: 所提出的SSA集成策略在CT-SDR中表现出优异的形状感知性能，验证了OFDR技术在柔性医疗器械中应用的可行性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel shape-sensing approach for Concentric Tube Steerable Drilling Robots (CT-SDRs) based on Optical Frequency Domain Reflectometry (OFDR). Unlike traditional FBG-based methods, OFDR enables continuous strain measurement along the entire fiber length with enhanced spatial resolution. In the proposed method, a Shape Sensing Assembly (SSA) is first fabricated by integrating a single OFDR fiber with a flat NiTi wire. The calibrated SSA is then routed through and housed within the internal channel of a flexible drilling instrument, which is guided by the pre-shaped NiTi tube of the CT-SDR. In this configuration, the drilling instrument serves as a protective sheath for the SSA during drilling, eliminating the need for integration or adhesion to the instrument surface that is typical of conventional optical sensor approaches. The performance of the proposed SSA, integrated within the cannulated CT-SDR, was thoroughly evaluated under free-bending conditions and during drilling along multiple J-shaped trajectories in synthetic Sawbones phantoms. Results demonstrate accurate and reliable shape-sensing capability, confirming the feasibility and robustness of this integration strategy.

</details>
